fk: (DEPARTMENT_ID, SECTION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT_SECTION(DEPARTMENT_ID, SECTION_ID)

## COUNTING_R [+audit] [+ml] — This table is in use of chemo administration sheets.
cols: COUNTING_ID NUMBER(3)

## COUNTRY_ZONE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)*; NAME VARCHAR2(100); NATIONALITY VARCHAR2(30); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1); REGION_ID VARCHAR2(4); OFFICE_ID NUMBER(3); CALLING_CODE VARCHAR2(5); ACTIVE CHAR(1); ISO_CC_2_DIGIT CHAR(2); WEB_VIEW CHAR(1)!; FINAL_DESTINATION VARCHAR2(1); ZONE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; NEW_COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(22)

## COURIER_CPT_DISTRICT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERIAL_NO NUMBER(5)!; COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)*; STATE_ID NUMBER(4)*; DISTRICT_ID NUMBER(4)*; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (COUNTRY_ID, STATE_ID, DISTRICT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DISTRICT(COUNTRY_ID, STATE_ID, DISTRICT_ID)
check: (ACTIVE IN('Y','N'))

## CPT_ADMIN_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(200); ADMIN_COSTING_ID VARCHAR2(8)

## CPT_ARCHIVE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «This column contains CPT ID»; DOCUMENT_TYPE_ID NUMBER «This column contains document TYPE id from HRD.DOCUMENT_TYPE table»; DOCUMENT_ID VARCHAR2(13)! «Year based Unique document ID from LOB.DOCUMENTS_STORE»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(2000) «other comments about document»; ATTACHED_BY VARCHAR2(14) «MRNO who attached file»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «For active 'Y' and 'N' for inactive»; SRNO NUMBER* «Serial Number»; ATTACHED_DATE DATE «Storage Date and Time when DOCUMENT is attached»

## CPT_BAN_TYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: BAN_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; BAN_TYPE_DESC VARCHAR2(100)!; MODULE_ID VARCHAR2(3)!

## CPT_BAN [+audit] [+ml]
cols: BAN_ID VARCHAR2(6)* «Unique BAN ID»; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)! «CPT ID foreign key reference to DEFINITIONS.CPT»; START_DATE DATE! «CPT BAN restriction appliable from»; END_DATE DATE «CPT BAN restriction expired on»; BAN_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)! «Module/Domain ID as BAN Type ID»; BAN_FACTOR CHAR(1)! «This BAN factor is appliable for details table D=disbale, B=BAN apply, E=Exampted»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000)! «Remarks display to user»
fk: (BAN_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_BAN_TYPES(BAN_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID)
uk: (CPT_ID, START_DATE)
check: (BAN_FACTOR in ('D','B','E'))

## CPT_BAN_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: BAN_ID VARCHAR2(6)* «BAN ID foreign reference to DEFINITIONS.CPT_BAN»; SERIAL_NO NUMBER(3)* «Unique Serial composite with BAN»; BAN_FACTOR CHAR(1)! «BAN Factor B=BAN Apply, E=BAN Exampted»; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3) «Location ID foreign key reference to DEFINITIONS.LOCATION»; ORDER_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3) «Order Type ID(e.g. 050=Inpatient, 001=Outpatient)»; PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6) «Patient Type ID (e.g. Ragular,Employee, Diagnostic etc.)»; PATIENT_MRNO VARCHAR2(14) «Patient MRNO foreign key reference to registration.patient»; CLIENT_ID VARCHAR2(10) «Company/Client ID»; USER_MRNO VARCHAR2(14) «User MRNO/Employee Code»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Active Status(Y=Yes,N=No)»; FORMER_MRNO VARCHAR2(14)
fk: (BAN_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_BAN(BAN_ID)
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID) [disabled]
fk: (PATIENT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE(PATIENT_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (PATIENT_MRNO) → REGISTRATION.PATIENT(MRNO) [disabled]
fk: (CLIENT_ID) → BILLING.CLIENT(CLIENT_ID) [disabled]
fk: (USER_MRNO) → SECURITY.USERS(MRNO) [disabled]
check: (BAN_FACTOR in ('B','E'))
check: (LENGTH(LOCATION_ID||'~'||ORDER_TYPE_ID||'~'||PATIENT_TYPE_ID||'~'||PATIENT_MRNO||'~'||CLIENT_ID||'~'||USER_MRNO)>5)

## CPT_BODY_REGION_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); SONMED_CODE VARCHAR2(20)

## CPT_CANCELLATION_REASON [+audit] [+ml]
cols: REASON_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE CHAR(1); SURGERY_APPT_REQ VARCHAR2(1) «This column will be used to store Flag Y If Surgery order required after cancellation of CPT - N In case when no surgery re-order required.»

## CPT_CATEGORY_COSTING [+audit] [+ml]

fk: (CPT_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_CATEGORY(CPT_CATEGORY_ID)
fk: (ADMIN_COSTING_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ADMIN_COSTING(ADMIN_COSTING_ID)

## CPT_CATEGORY_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_CATEGORY_DETAIL_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; CPT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(7); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (CPT_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_CATEGORY(CPT_CATEGORY_ID) [disabled]
check: ( ACTIVE IN ('Y', 'N'))

## CPT_CAT_PRICE_HISTORY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; SR_NO NUMBER(3)* «Revision Number»; NEW_PRICE NUMBER(12,2)! «Suggested/New Price of the CPT Category»; OLD_PRICE NUMBER(12,2)! «Current price of the CPT Category»; CHANGE_DATE DATE «Date on which this New Price Became Effective»; EFFECTIVE_DATE DATE! «Date on which new price should become Effective»
fk: (CPT_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_CATEGORY(CPT_CATEGORY_ID)

## CPT_CAT_COSTING_HISTORY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; SR_NO NUMBER(3)*; ADMIN_COSTING_ID VARCHAR2(8)*; NEW_PRICE NUMBER(12,2)!; OLD_PRICE NUMBER(12,2)!; NEW_QTY NUMBER(9,2)!; NEW_UNIT_ID CHAR(18); OLD_QTY NUMBER(9,2)!; OLD_UNIT_ID CHAR(18)
fk: (CPT_CATEGORY_ID, SR_NO) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_CAT_PRICE_HISTORY(CPT_CATEGORY_ID, SR_NO)
fk: (ADMIN_COSTING_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ADMIN_COSTING(ADMIN_COSTING_ID) [disabled]

## CPT_COSTING [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; ADMIN_COSTING_ID VARCHAR2(8)*; COST NUMBER(12,2)!; PERCENTAGE NUMBER(5,2)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID)
fk: (ADMIN_COSTING_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ADMIN_COSTING(ADMIN_COSTING_ID)

## CPT_PRICE_HISTORY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; NEW_PRICE NUMBER(12,2); CHANGE_DATE DATE; OLD_PRICE NUMBER(12,2)!; SR_NO NUMBER(3)*; EFFECTIVE_DATE DATE; CPT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(7); CPT_CATEGORY_SR_NO NUMBER(3); PROPOSAL_ID VARCHAR2(12); PROPOSAL_SRNO VARCHAR2(5); ACKNOWLEDGE CHAR(1) «If user acknowledge the CPT Price revison then system will allow to change the CPT Price.»

## CPT_COSTING_HISTORY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; CHANGE_DATE DATE!; SR_NO NUMBER(3)*; ADMIN_COSTING_ID VARCHAR2(8)*; NEW_COST NUMBER(12,2)!; OLD_COST NUMBER(12,2)!
fk: (CPT_ID, SR_NO) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_PRICE_HISTORY(CPT_ID, SR_NO)

## PACKAGE_TYPE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Package Category, This table is Constant Table
cols: PACKAGE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Primary key»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(120)! «Full Name of the Package Category»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(60)! «Short Name of the Package Category»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Package Category»
uk: (DESCRIPTION)
uk: (SHORT_DESC)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## PACKAGES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Primary key ( First Three characters will be location Id and remaining will be counter, Counter must be reset on new location_id)»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(100); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); PRICE NUMBER(12,2) «This column will represents the Total Price of the Package, Package price must be greater than zero»; COST NUMBER(12,2); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); PACKAGE_TYPE VARCHAR2(1); TRANS_DATE DATE!; HIDE_INVOICE_DETAIL CHAR(1) «IF THE COLUMN IS SET TO Y THEN WE WILL NOT SHOW THE CPTS DETAIL ON INVOICE. ONLY THE PACKAGE HEADING WILL BE SHOWN WITH TOTAL AMOUNT»; CPT_PRICE_SOURCE CHAR(1)! «This Column is added on requirement of Mr. Idrees, Currently we are getting CPT Price from CPT Table and Package Price from Package Table, Now for Package Price we will check from this Column if Values of this Column is P then get Price from Package else get price from CPT Table»; PACKAGE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3) «This column will be used to link the Package with the Package Category»; PRICE_SOURCE CHAR(1) «Values of this column will be P or I, P means Invoice will use the Item price from Package and I means Invoice will use the Item Price from their relevants tables/functions»; SHOW_ITEM_PRICE CHAR(1) «THIS COLUMN WILL BE USED TO SHOW ITEM PRICE OF PACKAGE WHEN Y»
fk: (PACKAGE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGE_TYPE(PACKAGE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]

## CPT_PACKAGE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)*; COST NUMBER(12,2); PRICE NUMBER(12,2); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); CPT_QTY NUMBER(3)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID)
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)

## CPT_PACKAGE_COSTING [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)*; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; ADMIN_COSTING_ID VARCHAR2(8)*; COST NUMBER(12,2)
fk: (PACKAGE_ID, CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_PACKAGE(PACKAGE_ID, CPT_ID) [disabled]
fk: (ADMIN_COSTING_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ADMIN_COSTING(ADMIN_COSTING_ID)

## CPT_PACKAGE_COSTING_TMP_R [+audit] [+ml]

## CPT_ROLE_REST_PERMIT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; ROLE_ID NUMBER(10)*; RESTRICTION_TYPE CHAR(1); ACTIVE CHAR(1); RESTRICTION_CATEGORY VARCHAR2(3)* «DUR mean Duration Restricted,PTR Patient Type Restriction»

## CPT_SCRIPT
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(250); NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3); CPT_TYPE VARCHAR2(1); PRICE NUMBER; NATURE_DETAIL_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## CPT_SPECIALTIES [+audit] — To link a CPT with multiple specialties of the same department
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; CLINIC_SPECIALITY_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000); WS_SYNC_DATE DATE
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID)
fk: (CLINIC_SPECIALITY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CLINIC_SPECIALITY(CLINIC_SPECIALITY_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))

## CPT_SPECIMEN [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; SPECIMEN_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1)! «This column contains flag information of default specimen during orderentry (Y=Yes,N=No)»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)! «This column contains flag information of active state (Y=Active,N=Inactive)»; REMARKS_REQUIRED VARCHAR2(1)!; LABEL_CLASS NUMBER(1)!; COLOR_DEFAULT CHAR(1)! «This column contains either default specimen color select automatically during reporting (Y=Yes, N=No)»; APPEARANCE_DEFAULT CHAR(1)! «This column contains either default specimen appearance select automatically during reporting (Y=Yes, N=No)»; PRINT_LABEL CHAR(1) «Label printing flag Y=Yes,N=No»; PRINT_COPIES NUMBER(2)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID)
fk: (SPECIMEN_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SPECIMEN(SPECIMEN_ID)
check: (DEFAULTS IN ('Y', 'N'))
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y', 'N'))
check: ((DEFAULTS = 'Y' AND ACTIVE = 'Y' OR DEFAULTS = 'N' AND ACTIVE = 'N' OR DEFAULTS = 'N' AND ACTIVE = 'Y'))
check: (REMARKS_REQUIRED in ('Y','N'))
check: (COLOR_DEFAULT in ('Y','N'))
check: (APPEARANCE_DEFAULT in ('Y','N'))
check: (PRINT_LABEL IN ('Y','N'))

## CPT_SURGERY_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(250); PRICE NUMBER(12,2)

## CPT_TEMP_MATIX_TABLE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); CHANGE_DATE DATE; OLD_PRICE NUMBER(22,2); NEW_PRICE NUMBER(22,2); DR_FEE_OLD NUMBER(22,2); DR_FEE_NEW NUMBER(22,2); ANES_SHARE_NEW NUMBER(22,2); ANES_SHARE_OLD NUMBER(22,2)

## CPT_UPD_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)!; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(250); CPT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(7); CPT_TYPE VARCHAR2(1); PRICE NUMBER(12,2); CPT_BONUS VARCHAR2(1); EMPLOYEE_ENTITLEMENT VARCHAR2(1); IN_HOUSE_PERFORMED VARCHAR2(1); COST NUMBER(12,2); DOCTOR_SHARE_TYPE VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(60); CONSULTANCY VARCHAR2(1); STAT_CHARGEABLE VARCHAR2(1); CLEARANCE VARCHAR2(1); MOST_COMMONLY_USED VARCHAR2(1); NO_OF_PROCEDURES NUMBER(2)!; REP_ORDER NUMBER(10)

## CPT_VARIABLE_HOLDER_SHARE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; ADMIN_COSTING_ID VARCHAR2(8)*; SHARE_HOLDER_ID VARCHAR2(14)*; START_DATE DATE*; END_DATE DATE; SHARE_PERCENTAGE NUMBER(5,2); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (CPT_ID, ADMIN_COSTING_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT_COSTING(CPT_ID, ADMIN_COSTING_ID)
fk: (SHARE_HOLDER_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SHARE_HOLDER(SHARE_HOLDER_ID)

## CPT_VIEW [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; VIEW_ID NUMBER*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## CPT_WISE_DISTRICT_REST [+audit]
cols: SR_NO NUMBER*; COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)!; STATE_ID NUMBER(4)!; DISTRICT_ID NUMBER(4)!; NAME VARCHAR2(60); CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## CPT_WISE_DOCUMENT_TYPE [+audit]
cols: DOC_TYPE_ID NUMBER* «This column contain document ID active flag.»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); AUTO_ATTACH_ALLOWED VARCHAR2(1)

cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «This column contain unique CPT ID.»; SR_NO NUMBER* «This column contain SR #.»; DOC_TYPE_ID NUMBER* «This column contain document ID.»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000) «This column contain remarks against document ID.»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1) «This column contain active flag.»; LOCATION_WISE_REST VARCHAR2(1); WS_SYNC_DATE DATE; PATIENT_TYPE_REST VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID)
uk: (CPT_ID, DOC_TYPE_ID)

## CPT_WISE_PATIENT_TYPE_REST [+audit]
cols: SR_NO NUMBER*; DOC_TYPE_ID NUMBER*; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(18); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000)

cols: SETUP_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; RESTRICTION_TYPE_ID NUMBER(4); RESTRICTION_ID NUMBER(4); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## CPT_WISE_STATE_REST [+audit]
cols: SR_NO NUMBER*; COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)!; STATE_ID NUMBER(4)!; NAME VARCHAR2(60); CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); WS_SYNC_DATE DATE

## CPT_WISE_USER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; EMPLOYEE_CODE VARCHAR2(14)* «PAT_CONSULTANT,PAT_DOCTOR AND PAT_TECH employees to verify CPT»; START_DATE DATE «Starting date to verify Cpt verification»; END_DATE DATE «Ending date to verify Cpt verification»

## CURRENCY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CURRENCY_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); SYMBOL VARCHAR2(3); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1); COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4); SHORT_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(3); CURRENT_EXCHANGE_RATE NUMBER(7,4); MARKETING_EXCHANGE_RATE NUMBER(7,4); MARKETING_SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(3)

## DATA_CHECKING_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: BATCH_NO VARCHAR2(9); TABLE_NAME VARCHAR2(60); COUNT_IMPORTED NUMBER; COUNT_EXPORTED NUMBER

## DATA_NOTES_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: A NUMBER(10); B VARCHAR2(4000); C VARCHAR2(4000); D VARCHAR2(4000); E VARCHAR2(4000); F VARCHAR2(4000); G VARCHAR2(4000); H VARCHAR2(4000); I VARCHAR2(4000); J VARCHAR2(4000); K VARCHAR2(4000); L VARCHAR2(4000); M VARCHAR2(4000); N VARCHAR2(4000)

## DATA_SYNC_ERROR_MODEL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: RULE_ID VARCHAR2(6)* «This column contain rule, this column will be primary key»; QUEUE_NAME VARCHAR2(250) «This column contain queue name EMR Queue,EMR Queue detail and Except EMR»; DML_EVENT VARCHAR2(30) «This column contain Insert,Delete and Update»; ERROR VARCHAR2(4000) «This column contain Error»; ROWS_AFFECTED VARCHAR2(30) «This column contain affected rows»; QUEUE_STATUS CHAR(1) «This column contain queue status like E=Error,S=Sucess»; ACTION_FOR_PROCEDURE VARCHAR2(100) «This column contain action for procedure»; MAIN_PROCEDURE VARCHAR2(500) «This column contain main componet of sub procedure»; SUB_PROCEDURE VARCHAR2(500) «This column contain value of sub procedure»; FIND_PARENT CHAR(1) «This column contain action for parent record Y=Yes,N=No»; FIND_CHILD CHAR(1) «This column contain action for child record Y=Yes,N=No»; CONVERT_DML CHAR(1) «This column contain regenerate DML Y=Yes,N=No»; INSERT_HISTORY CHAR(1) «This column contain insert row history table Y=Yes,N=No»; INCREASE_ATTEMPTS CHAR(1) «This column contain increase no of movement attempts Y=Yes,N=No»; UPD_LAST_ERROR CHAR(1) «This column contain update last error Y=Yes,N=No»; ERROR_IN_TABLE VARCHAR2(150) «This column contain save error in table like admin queue»; EMR_Q_INS CHAR(1) «This column contain emr queue insert Y=Yes,N=No»; EMR_Q_DETAIL_INS CHAR(1) «This column contain emr queue insert in detail Y=Yes,N=No»; DELETE_FROM_Q CHAR(1) «This column contain remove for actuall queue Y=Yes,N=No»; INFORM_ADMIN CHAR(1) «This column contain inform to admin insert row in admin queue Y=Yes,N=No»; STOP_PROCESSING CHAR(1) «This column contain processing stop Y=Yes,N=No»; ERROR_CODE VARCHAR2(100) «This column contain Error oracle and customize code»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000)
uk: (QUEUE_NAME, DML_EVENT, ERROR)

## DATA_SYNC_MODEL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DB_USER VARCHAR2(30)* «This column contain value of data model sync»; DB_TYPE VARCHAR2(10)* «This column contain value of data model data type»; NETWORK_TOPOLOGY VARCHAR2(30)* «This column contain value of data model network toplogy»; ACTION_FOR_TIGGER VARCHAR2(30) «This column contain value of data model data action for trigger»; ACTION_FOR_PROCEDURE VARCHAR2(30) «This column contain value of data model data action for procedure»; MODEL_TYPE_ID NUMBER(2)* «This column contain value of data model type id»
check: (NETWORK_TOPOLOGY IN ('STAR','MESH'))
check: (ACTION_FOR_TIGGER IN ('NONE','DC-TO-CR','DC-TO-ALL'))
check: (ACTION_FOR_PROCEDURE IN ('NONE','DC-TO-ALL','DC-TO-BUFFER'))

## DATA_SYNC_MODEL_TYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MODEL_TYPE_ID NUMBER(2)* «This column contain values of Model type id»; DESCRIPTIONS VARCHAR2(200) «This column contain values of data sync model name»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(100) «This column contain values of data sync model short desc»; ACTIVE CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(500)

## DATES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DATE_COLUMN DATE*
check: (DATE_COLUMN = TRUNC(DATE_COLUMN))

## DAY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DAY_ID NUMBER(1)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(10); STATUS VARCHAR2(1); NORMAL_FROM_TIME VARCHAR2(5)!; NORMAL_TO_TIME VARCHAR2(5)!; RAMZAN_FROM_TIME VARCHAR2(5)!; RAMZAN_TO_TIME VARCHAR2(5)!; WORKING_DAY CHAR(1)!
check: (WORKING_DAY IN ('Y','N'))

## DAY_LANGUAGE_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LANGUAGE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DAY_ID NUMBER(1)* «Key of DEFINITIONS.DAY»; LABEL_DESC NVARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## DAY_MINUTES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MINUTE_NUMBER NUMBER(4)*

## DAY_SESSION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SESSION_ID NUMBER*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(50); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## DAY_SESSION_LANG_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]

## DAY_WISE_RESOURCE_SCHEDULE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DAY_ID NUMBER(1)*; RESOURCE_ID NUMBER(4)*; AVAILABLE_FROM DATE; AVAILABLE_TO DATE; OVER_BOOKING_MINUTES NUMBER(4); OVER_BOOKING CHAR(1); EMERGENCY_BED CHAR(1); LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## DB_SERVICES_MODE [+audit]
cols: DISTRIBUTED_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); SERVICES_MODE CHAR(1) «D for distributed, S for waiting for syncing»; START_DATE DATE; END_DATE DATE

## DB_USERS [+audit] [+ml] — The purpose of this table is to store the information of all database users and to identify them by types i.e. system users or application users etc.
cols: USERNAME VARCHAR2(60)* «Database user name»; CREATED DATE «Store the creation date of database user»; USER_TYPE CHAR(1) «identify the type of users i.e. "S" for system users, "A" for application users i.e. registration,orderentry etc., "U" for MIS users and "D" for auditing user "C" for connection users»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «value "Y" shows that user is active and "N" shows that user is inactive or leave the organization»

## DC_MEDICINE_FILTERS [+audit] — This table is used to store then setup data of medicine filters.
cols: FILTER_ID NUMBER!; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(50) «This column is used to store the short description of medicine filter.»; ORDER_BY NUMBER «This column is used to store the order by value.»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1) «Y - Active, N - Inactive.»; VALUE NUMBER; DEFAULT_VALUE VARCHAR2(1)

## DECISION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DECISION_ID VARCHAR2(10)*; DECISION_DESC VARCHAR2(100); DECISION_SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(10); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## FILES_SERVERS_TYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERVER_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; TYPE_DESC VARCHAR2(100)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Y: ACTIVE, N: INACTIVE»
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## FILES_SERVERS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERVER_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Unique key of server ID 000=Location ID + YY=Current Year + 99999=Counter)»; SERVER_NAME VARCHAR2(100)! «This column contains unique server name»; ACCESS_PATH VARCHAR2(1000)! «This column contains full qualifying acess path of machine»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(200) «Remarks note»; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)! «This column contains row creator object code»; CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(20)! «This column contains row creator user information»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag column (Y=active, N=Inactive)»; SERVER_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «This column contains Server Type ID reference to DEFINITIONS.SERVER_TYPES»; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «This column contains Physical Location ID of server (e.g. 001,007,C01 etc)»
fk: (SERVER_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FILES_SERVERS_TYPES(SERVER_TYPE_ID)
uk: (ACCESS_PATH)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## FILES_TYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FILE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Unique ID for File Type ID»; FILE_DESC VARCHAR2(60)! «Description of File Description»; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(100); STORAGE_TYPE VARCHAR2(2) «Storage type is used to preserve document (DB=database, OS=Operating system)»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag column to contain status Y=Active,N=Inactive»; DEFAULT_TEMPORARY_PATH VARCHAR2(1000) «This column contains default temporary path to copy the attached file on the allocated default server»; OPEN_WITH VARCHAR2(3); FILE_SIZE NUMBER(8,2) «This column contains the uploading file maximum size limit in MBs. Null or zero means no limit on size of uploading file»; DELETE_SOURCE_FILE CHAR(1) «This column contains the flag value (Y/N)»; OPEN_FILE_AFTER_DOWNLOAD CHAR(1) «This column contains the flag value (Y/N) to decide whether the file to be opend after downloading?»
uk: (FILE_DESC)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## DEFAULT_FILES_SERVERS [+audit] [+ml] — This table is used to assign servers to specific locations according to the modality and server type. Server Priority is also assigned
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Location ID for which the server setting is being made.»; SERVER_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Server Type ID for the Location and File_Type_ID for which the server setting is being made.»; SERVER_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Server ID assigned to the specified location according to File_Type_ID and Server Type.»; PRIORITY NUMBER(2)! «Priority of the assigned server»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Y:Active, N:Inactive»; FILE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*
fk: (SERVER_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FILES_SERVERS_TYPES(SERVER_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (SERVER_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FILES_SERVERS(SERVER_ID) [disabled]
fk: (FILE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FILES_TYPES(FILE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
uk: (PRIORITY, SERVER_TYPE_ID, LOCATION_ID, FILE_TYPE_ID)

## DEFAULT_MEDICINE_ORDER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; MEDICAL_ITEM VARCHAR2(1)*; FREQUENCY_ID VARCHAR2(5); DURATION NUMBER(6,2); DURATION_UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(5); ROUTE_ID VARCHAR2(5)

## DEFAULT_REPORT_DESTINATION [+audit] [+ml]

cols: TRANSACTION_NO VARCHAR2(9)*; TRANSACTION_DATE DATE; BED_ID VARCHAR2(7); ROOM_ID VARCHAR2(7); CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(6); CURRENT_EVENT_ID VARCHAR2(3); CURRENT_EVENT_DATE DATE; CURRENT_PERFORM_BY VARCHAR2(14); OLD_EVENT_ID VARCHAR2(3); OLD_EVENT_DATE DATE; OLD_PERFORM_BY VARCHAR2(14); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000); LOC_ID VARCHAR2(3)*

cols: CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; CATEGORY_TYPE_ID NUMBER(5)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(25); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); REFERENCE_NO VARCHAR2(2); WS_SYNC_DATE DATE
fk: (CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEF_CATEGORY(CATEGORY_ID)

## DEF_CPT_PROP_WORKFLOW

cols: PARAMETER_ID NUMBER(2)*; PARAMETER_VALUE VARCHAR2(100)*; DISPLAY_ORDER NUMBER(2); DEFAULT_VALUE CHAR(1); ACTIVE CHAR(1); LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*
fk: (PARAMETER_ID, ORGANIZATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEF_DP_PARAMETER(PARAMETER_ID, ORGANIZATION_ID) [disabled]

cols: ID NUMBER(4)*; TREATMENT_NAME VARCHAR2(50)!; TREATMENT_DESC VARCHAR2(500); IS_ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## DEF_FLAGS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FLAG_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; FLAG_DESC VARCHAR2(100)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1); REVIEW_DURATION NUMBER «Review duration will be the first time when patient is marked and alert will appear after review duration, its value is in days»; REPEAT_REVIEW_DURATION NUMBER «Repeat Review duration will be used to repear the duration, its value is in days»; AUTO_REMOVAL_DURATION NUMBER «Review duration will be used to auto cancel the results, its value is in days»; RESTRICT_REMOVAL_DURATION NUMBER «This column will be used to apply restriction to remove flag on Patient flag screen. It will be in days»

## DEF_FLAGS_ALERT_ROLES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SR_NO NUMBER* «SR# will be generated.»; FLAG_ID VARCHAR2(5)* «Flag id i.e. COVID.»; ROLE_ID NUMBER(20) «Role id against which alert will be generated.»; ALERT VARCHAR2(1) «Role alert flag will be used for alert.»; REVIEW VARCHAR2(1) «Role review flag will be used to review the alert.»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1) «Active/In-active.»

## DEF_INSTRUCTIONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: INSTRUCTION_ID NUMBER*; INSTRUCTION_PRE_TEXT VARCHAR2(500); INSTRUCTION_POST_TEXT VARCHAR2(500); REMARKS VARCHAR2(500); INSTRUCTION_TYPE VARCHAR2(1) «S-> STATIC, D-> DYNAMIC (PARAMETERS WILL BE DEFINED)»; ORDER_BY NUMBER; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## DEF_INSTRUCTIONS_LANG_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERIAL_NO NUMBER; INSTRUCTION_ID NUMBER; LANGUAGE_ID VARCHAR2(6); LABEL_DESC NVARCHAR2(500); LABEL_DESC1 NVARCHAR2(500); LABEL_DESC2 NVARCHAR2(500); LABEL_DESC3 NVARCHAR2(500); LABEL_DESC4 NVARCHAR2(500); LABEL_DESC5 NVARCHAR2(500); LABEL_DESC6 NVARCHAR2(500); LABEL_DESC7 NVARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## DEF_MRN_TRAN_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MRN_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(1)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(50)!; TRANS_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)!; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## SERVICE_CATEGORY_GROUP [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Service Category Group e.g. CPT, Pharmacy, Consultation, Supplies etc. Data of this table is Constant
cols: SERVICE_CATEGORY_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Counter of the Service Category Group which will start from 001, however this value is constant»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)! «Name of the Service Category Group»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
check: (DESCRIPTION=UPPER(DESCRIPTION))
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ORG_ID='SKM')

cols: SERVICE_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Counter of the Table which will start from 001»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)! «Name of the Service Category»; SERVICE_CATEGORY_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «Service Category e.g. C for CPT, T for Consultation»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_CATEGORY_GROUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_CATEGORY_GROUP(SERVICE_CATEGORY_GROUP_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

uk: (TR_SHORT_DESC)

cols: VAL VARCHAR2(3000)

## DEPARTMENT_NATURE_ARCHIVE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains inforamation department Nature ID (reference: DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT_NATURE)»; NATURE_DETAIL_ID VARCHAR2(3) «This column contains Detail ID of department Nature ID (Unique Nature+Serial)»; DOCUMENT_TYPE_ID NUMBER «This column contains document TYPE id from HRD.DOCUMENT_TYPE table»; DOCUMENT_ID VARCHAR2(13) «Year based Unique document ID from LOB.DOCUMENTS_STORE»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(2000) «other comments about document»; ATTACHED_BY VARCHAR2(14) «MRNO who attached file»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «For active 'Y' and 'N' for inactive»; SRNO NUMBER* «Serial Number»; ATTACHED_DATE DATE «Storage Date and Time when DOCUMENT is attached»

fk: (DEPARTMENT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT(DEPARTMENT_ID)

fk: (ROLE_ID) → SECURITY.ROLE(ROLE_ID) [disabled]

fk: (DESIGNATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DESIGNATION(DESIGNATION_ID) [disabled]

## DESIG_CATEGOTY_WISE_CPTS

cols: DESIGNATION_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; SUB_DESIG_CAT_ID VARCHAR2(3)*

fk: (SUB_DESIG_CAT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DESIGNATION_CATEGORY(DESIGNATION_CATEGORY_ID) [disabled]

## DESIG_SPECIALITY_PRIVILEGES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CLINIC_SPECIALITY_ID VARCHAR2(6)! «Clinical Speciality ID from DEFINITIONS.CLINIC_SPECIALITY»; DESIGNATION_ID VARCHAR2(6)! «Designation ID from DEFINITIONS.DESIGNATION»; ON_PROBATION CHAR(1)! «Either Y or N»; PRIVILEGES_ID NUMBER «Privileges ID from DEFINITIONS.CONSULTANT_PRIVILEGES_SETUP»; DOCUMENT_ID VARCHAR2(13) «DOCUMENT_ID from LOB.DOCUMENTS_STORE»; DISPLAY CHAR(1) «Either Privilege Document should display or Not»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «Either Y or N»; ATTACHED_BY VARCHAR2(14) «USER_ID who aatcahed Document»; ATTACHMENT_DATE DATE «Date on which document was attached»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); SERIAL_NO NUMBER(8)*
fk: (CLINIC_SPECIALITY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CLINIC_SPECIALITY(CLINIC_SPECIALITY_ID) [disabled]
fk: (PRIVILEGES_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CONSULTANT_PRIVILEGES_SETUP(PRIVILEGES_ID) [disabled]
fk: (DOCUMENT_ID) → LOB.DOCUMENTS_STORE(DOCUMENT_ID) [disabled]
fk: (ATTACHED_BY) → HRD.INFORMATION(MRNO) [disabled]
fk: (DESIGNATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DESIGNATION(DESIGNATION_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ON_PROBATION IN ('Y','N'))
check: (DISPLAY IN ('Y','N'))

## DIAGNOSIS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DIAGNOSIS_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; PRIMARY_REGIMENS NUMBER(2)!; RELAPSED_REGIMENS NUMBER(2)!; ICD_ID VARCHAR2(11); PATIENT_TO_BE_EXAMINED_BY CHAR(1)! «IT MAY CONTAIN VALUES LIKE R/F/O "R" FOR RESIDENT, "F" FOR FELLOW AND "O" FOR ONCOLOGIST»
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y', 'N'))
check: (PATIENT_TO_BE_EXAMINED_BY IN ('R','F','O'))

fk: (OBJECT_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE) [disabled]

## ICD [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ICDNO VARCHAR2(11)*; ICD_NO VARCHAR2(11); SHORT_EQUAL VARCHAR2(1); ICD_NO_4 VARCHAR2(5); ICD_NO_3 VARCHAR2(3); ESTIMATED_COST NUMBER(10,2); ESTIMATED_DURATION NUMBER(4); ADDITIONAL_ICD VARCHAR2(1)!; LONG_DESC VARCHAR2(250)!; ICD_CHAPTER_ID VARCHAR2(3); ICD_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(3); ICDO_3_DESC VARCHAR2(45); ICD_TYPE VARCHAR2(25)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; ICDNO_FOR_CANCER CHAR(1)!; BACK_GROUND_DIAGNOSIS CHAR(1)!; FU_FOR_LIFE CHAR(1) «Y mean for life time follow up treatment»; VERSION NUMBER(2)*; ICD10_SERIAL_NO VARCHAR2(10)!; ICD_O3 CHAR(1) «Y mean ICD for O3 and N mean ICD not for O3»; REPORTABLE CHAR(1)! «Y mean Reportable & N mean not Reportable»; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)
check: (ADDITIONAL_ICD IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ICD_TYPE IN ('SURGICAL PROCEDURE','DIAGNOSTIC CODE','PROCEDURE','E CODE','V CODE'))
check: (ICDNO_FOR_CANCER IN ('N','Y'))

## DIAGNOSIS_REPORT_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]

fk: (SETUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DIAGNOSIS_REPORT_SETUP(SETUP_ID)
fk: (ICDNO, VERSION, LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ICD(ICDNO, VERSION, LOCATION_ID) [disabled]

cols: SETUP_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; USER_DEPARTMENT VARCHAR2(7) «Login user doctor department, doctor which will edit admission request.»; USER_CLINIC_SPECIALITY VARCHAR2(6) «Login user doctor admitting speciality, doctor which will edit admission request.»; ADMITTING_DOCTOR_DEPARTMENT VARCHAR2(7) «admitting doctor department against admission request enter.»; ADMITTING_DR_CLINIC_SPECIALITY VARCHAR2(6) «admitting doctor sepciality against admission request enter.»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(500)!; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); EDIT_TYPE VARCHAR2(1)! «D= Allow to edit admission request by comparing user departmnt and admitting doctor department ,S= Allow to edit admission request by comparing user clinic sepciality and admitting doctor clinic speciality»; RESTRICTION_TYPE VARCHAR2(1)! «A= Allow to edit admission request, R= Restrict to edit admission request.»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)!; LOC_ID VARCHAR2(3); ZON_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORG_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## EFFECT_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: EFFECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ET_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## EMAIL_ALERT [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains information of list of Email Events
cols: LIST_ID VARCHAR2(5)* «Unique key of Email List ID»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500) «This column contains information of description of Email Events»; EMAIL_SUBJECT VARCHAR2(160) «This column contains information of Email Subject»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column contains flag information of activate status (Y=Active, N=Inactive)»; EMAIL_SENDER VARCHAR2(50) «This column contains email address of sender»

## EMAIL_ALERT_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LIST_ID VARCHAR2(5)* «Auto generated number.»; EMAIL_SERIAL VARCHAR2(60)* «Auto generated number.»; RECIPIENT_MRNO VARCHAR2(14) «Email recipient's MRNO number.»; RECIPIENT_EMAIL VARCHAR2(60) «Email recipient's email address..»; SENDER_MRNO VARCHAR2(14) «Email sender's MRNO number.»; SENDER_EMAIL VARCHAR2(60) «Email senders's email address..»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «Flag to mark if the record is Active or not.»; MOBILE_NUMBER VARCHAR2(100) «SMS recipient's mobile number.»; SEND_SMS_YN CHAR(1) «Flag to mark if the SMS should be sent or not.»; EMAIL_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3) «This column contains Email Location ID»
fk: (LIST_ID) → DEFINITIONS.EMAIL_ALERT(LIST_ID)
check: (SEND_SMS_YN IN ('Y','N'))

## EMPLOYER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: EMPLOYER_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); ACTIVE CHAR(1)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## EMP_TRAINING_DOCUMENT_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DOC_TYPE_ID NUMBER* «DOCUMENT TYPE WILL BE STORED HERE»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500) «DOCUMENT TYPE DESCRIPTION WILL BE STORED HERE»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «VALUES Y OR N»; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(20)

## EMP_WISE_CPTS
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; EMP_CODE VARCHAR2(14)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); FROM_DATE DATE; TO_DATE DATE

## EMP_WISE_CPTS_EXEMPT
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; EMP_CODE VARCHAR2(14)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); FROM_DATE DATE; TO_DATE DATE

## EMR_TABLES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: OWNER VARCHAR2(20)*; TABLE_NAME VARCHAR2(50)*; COLUMN_NAME VARCHAR2(30)*; DATA_TYPE VARCHAR2(20); SAVE_ORIGINAL_VALUE CHAR(1); ORDER_BY NUMBER; ACTIVE CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(2000); DEFAULT_WHERE VARCHAR2(2000); INSERT_REQUIRED CHAR(1) «IF value = Y , it means we have to insert record first in master table and then update data in child table»; IS_PARENT_TABLE CHAR(1) «this column is used to mark parent/master table»; PARENT_OWNER_NAME VARCHAR2(20)! «this column is used for parent/master owner name»; PARENT_TABLE_NAME VARCHAR2(100)! «this column is used for parent/master table name»

## ENCOUNTER_TREATMENT [+audit]
cols: EN_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; TR_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (EN_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEF_ENCOUNTER_CATEGORY(EN_CATEGORY_ID)
fk: (TR_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEF_TREATMENT_CATEGORY(TR_CATEGORY_ID)

## ENCRYPTED_TABLES [+audit]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; TABLE_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; NAME VARCHAR2(60)!; WS_SYNC_DATE DATE; ENCRYPTION_ALLOWED CHAR(1)

## ENCRYPTED_TABLE_COLUMNS [+audit]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; TABLE_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; COLUMN_NAME VARCHAR2(30); COLUMN_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; DATA_TYPE VARCHAR2(30); ENCRYPTION_ALLOWED CHAR(1); ENCRYPTION_KEY VARCHAR2(500)

## ENTITY_TYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ENTITY_TYPE_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255)!; ENTITY_SOURCE_TABLE VARCHAR2(255) «Store entity type source table name ex/ REGISTRATION.PATIENT»; ENTITY_SOURCE_COLUMN VARCHAR2(255) «Store source table key column name ex/ MRNO»; ENTITY_COLUMN_VALUE VARCHAR2(255) «Store column name to get entity value name/description ex/ NAME»
uk: (DESCRIPTION)

## ERROR_MESSAGES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ERROR_ID NUMBER*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)

## ESI_PRIORITY [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to save ESI Priority
cols: PRIORITY_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100) «this column is used to save priority description»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(3) «this column is used to save priority short description»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(2) «Y - Yes, N - No»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## EVENT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: EVENT_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); ACTIVE CHAR(1); EMAIL_SUBJECT VARCHAR2(100); EMAIL_BODY VARCHAR2(500); USER_RUNTIME VARCHAR2(1); OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11) «Column for Calling Event Object Code»; SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3); COLOR_CODE VARCHAR2(20); SHORT_DESC CHAR(4); OUTPUT VARCHAR2(1); NOTE_ENTRY VARCHAR2(1); LABEL_DESC VARCHAR2(255); RESTRICT_REPEAT_EVENT VARCHAR2(1) «This column is used to restrict the repetition of events»; SEND_EMAIL CHAR(1) «This column contains flag to use email solution for specific event»; REMINDER_DAYS NUMBER «This column is used to save no of days for first reminder»; NEXT_REMINDER_DAYS NUMBER «This column is used to save no of days for second reminder»; REMINDER_FREQUENCY NUMBER «This column is used to save alert frequency, how many time alert will be generate»; CUSTOM_REMINDER_DAYS CHAR(1) «This column is used to save a flag for customizr reminder days(manually enter by any authority)»
check: (USER_RUNTIME IN ('N','Y'))

## EVENT_DECISION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PURCHASE_TYPE_ID NUMBER*; WORK_FLOW_ID NUMBER(4)*; SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; EVENT_ID NUMBER(3)*; DECISION_ID NUMBER(3)*; ORDER_STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## EVENT_GROUPS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains CPT Nature ID e.g. 096=Lab, 029, radiology, 030=NM (Reference to DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT_NATURE)»; EVENT_ID NUMBER(4)* «This column contains Event ID (Reference to DEFINITIONS.EVENT)»; STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «This column contains Order Status ID (e.g. 001=Order, 002 Invoiced, 019=Under process, 015 complete)»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(2)! «This column contains order by thsi can be used to display events in reports»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contains active status of Event group(Y=Active, N=Inactive)»; LOG_EVENT CHAR(1)! «This column contains Flag Information to log Event (O=Order CPT, D=Module/Detail)»; LOG_DEPT CHAR(1)! «This column contains Flag Information to log Department (C=CPT, U=User, O=Order)»
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (LOG_EVENT in ('O','D'))
check: (LOG_DEPT in ('C','U','O'))

## EVENT_USER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PURCHASE_TYPE_ID NUMBER*; WORK_FLOW_ID NUMBER(4)*; EVENT_ID NUMBER(3)*; MRNO VARCHAR2(14)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «HIS MODULE_ID»; FORMER_MRNO VARCHAR2(14)

## EVENT_WISE_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PARAMETER_ID NUMBER(3)* «This column contains parameter id (Reference from definitions.event_wise_parameter)»; PURCHASE_TYPE_ID NUMBER* «This column contains purchase type id (Reference from mms.def_purchase_type)»; WORK_FLOW_ID NUMBER(4)* «This column contains work flow id (Reference from definitions.pr_type_flow)»; EVENT_ID NUMBER(3)* «This column contains event id (Reference from definitions.pr_type_flow_event)»; SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains schema id (Reference from definitions.schemas)»; PARAMETER_VALUE VARCHAR2(150) «This column contains parameter value (Reference from definitions.event_wise_parameter)»; EMAIL CHAR(1) «This column contains flag of email in Y/ N format»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(3) «This column contains execution order of parameters»; AUTO_PERFORM CHAR(1) «This column contains flag of auto perform»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column contains flag of active»

## EVENT_WISE_PARAMETER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PARAMETER_ID NUMBER(3)* «This column contains parameter id in number format.»; PARAMETER_DESC VARCHAR2(150) «This column contains parameter description.»; PARAMETER_TYPE VARCHAR2(5) «This column contains parameter type.»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column contains ACTIVE flag.»

## EXCEPTIONAL_LEAVE_HIERARCHY [+audit] [+ml]

fk: (LEAVE_ROLE_ID) → HRD.LEAVE_ROLE(LEAVE_ROLE_ID) [disabled]

## EXCEPTION_TYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contain value of Exception types»; EXCEPTION_DESC VARCHAR2(500) «This column contain value of Exception description»; CODE_ERROR VARCHAR2(100) «This column contain value of Exception code»

## EXPENSE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: EXPENSE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)!; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1)

## EXTERNAL_DOCTOR_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DOCTOR_ID VARCHAR2(10)*; DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; SECTION_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; CONTRACT_STRT_DATE DATE; CONTRACT_END_DATE DATE; TEST_REQ_FOR_BONUS NUMBER; BONUS NUMBER; TOT_TESTS NUMBER; BONUS_DUE NUMBER; BONUS_AVAILED NUMBER; BONUS_BAL NUMBER
fk: (DOCTOR_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DOCTOR_EXTERNAL(DOCTOR_ID)

## EXTRA_SKILLS_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: EXTRA_SKILL_ID VARCHAR2(5); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE CHAR(1); DEFAULTS CHAR(1)

## FIELD_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FIELD_TYPE_ID NUMBER*; NAME VARCHAR2(100)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1); APEX_NAME VARCHAR2(100)

## FILES_EXTENSIONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: EXT_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Unique ID for specific File Extension»; EXT_DESC VARCHAR2(5)! «Unique Extension Description»; ORDER_BY NUMBER «Order by numbers»; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag column to contain status Y=Active,N=Inactive»
uk: (EXT_DESC)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## FILES_SERVERS_SYNC_SETUP [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains Servers Replication Setup for Attached/Uploaded FILES
cols: SOURCE_SERVER_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Source server ID; study is being copied from»; TARGET_SERVER_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Destination server ID; study is being copied to»; ACCESS_MODE VARCHAR2(1)! «Flag Information (L=LAN, W=WAN)»; PRIORITY NUMBER(2)! «request processing priority sequence»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag Y=Active, N=Inactive»; START_DATE DATE! «Start date of Activation»; END_DATE DATE «End/Epiiry date of Activation»; SYNC_MODE CHAR(1)! «Flag Information (N=No, A=Automatic, M=Manual)»
fk: (SOURCE_SERVER_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FILES_SERVERS(SERVER_ID)
fk: (TARGET_SERVER_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FILES_SERVERS(SERVER_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACCESS_MODE in ('L','W'))
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (SYNC_MODE in ('N','A','M'))

## FILES_TEMPORARY_PATHS [+audit] [+ml] — This is a setup table FILES_TEMPORARY_PATHS contains setup values for all location with temporary physical paths for temporarily placing the file(s)/document(s) for archiving purpose at differenct physical paths
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column conatins Location Id, Reference:DEFINITIONS.LOCATION.LOCATION_ID»; PHYSICAL_PATH VARCHAR2(500)! «This column conatins temporary physical paths defined for the file(s)/document(s) to be placed on temporarily, which will be moved on permanent physical paths»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column contains Flag (N=No, Y=Yes)»
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)

## FILES_TYPES_ASSOCIATE_WITH [+audit] [+ml] — It is setup table; and contains Associated Files Type
cols: FTAW_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «It contains PK Id Unique ID for File Type ID»; FILE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «Unique ID for File Type ID»; FILE_DESC VARCHAR2(100)! «Description of File Description»; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(500) «Description of File Description»; PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6) «Description of File Description»; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3) «Description of File Description»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag column to contain status Y=Active,N=Inactive»
uk: (FILE_DESC)

## FILES_TYPES_ASSOCIATE_WITH_DET [+audit] [+ml] — It is setup table; and contains Associated Files Type
cols: FTAW_DET_ID VARCHAR2(10)*; FTAW_ID VARCHAR2(10)! «It contains PK Id Unique ID for File Type ID»; FILE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «Unique ID for File Type ID»; DOCUMENT_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(200); COMPULSORY_ATTACHMENT CHAR(1)!; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(500) «Description of File Description»; ORDER_BY NUMBER; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag column to contain status Y=Active,N=Inactive»

## FILES_TYPES_DET [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Unique ID for specific File Type Id»; FILE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Foreign Key reference column of Table DEFINITIONS.FILES_TYPES»; TYPE_DESC VARCHAR2(10)! «Description of specific file type»; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag column to contain status Y=Active,N=Inactive»; STORAGE_TYPE VARCHAR2(2) «Storage type is used to preserve document (DB=database, OS=Operating system)»; COUNT_PAGES CHAR(1)! «Flag column to contain status Y=Yes,N=No»; OPEN_WITH VARCHAR2(3); FILE_SIZE NUMBER(8,2) «This column contains the uploading file maximum size limit in MBs. Null or zero means no limit on size of uploading file»; DELETE_SOURCE_FILE CHAR(1) «This column contains the flag value (Y/N)»; FILE_TYPE_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500) «This column is used for file(s) filteration using extension(s)»; OPEN_FILE_AFTER_DOWNLOAD CHAR(1) «This column contains the flag value (Y/N) to decide whether the file to be opend after downloading?»
fk: (FILE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FILES_TYPES(FILE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
uk: (FILE_TYPE_ID, TYPE_DESC)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## FILES_VIEWERS [+audit] [+ml] — This is setup table and is used to define softwares to be used to view specific files e.g. *.chm
cols: VIEWER_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Unique ID for File Viewer ID»; VIEWER_NAME VARCHAR2(60)! «Complete Name of the Viewer which will be used to view the file»; VIEWER_PATH VARCHAR2(1000) «Complete Path where the Viewer is installed, and will be used to view the file»; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(1000); ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Flag column to contain status Y=Active,N=Inactive»
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## FILE_UPLOADS
cols: ID NUMBER; FILE_NAME VARCHAR2(255); FILE_CONTENT BLOB; FILE_TEXT CLOB; UPLOAD_DATE DATE

fk: (FILM_CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID) [disabled]

## FINANCIAL_YEAR_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FROM_DATE DATE; TO_DATE DATE; ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## FONT_ATTRIBUTE_OBJECT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ATTRIBUTE_ID NUMBER(8)*; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(500)

## FOOD_ALERGIES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FOOD_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## FORMATE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FORMATE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; NAME VARCHAR2(200); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## FREQUENCY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FREQUENCY_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); SHORT_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(12); FREQUENCY_VALUE NUMBER(7,2); LABLE_DESC VARCHAR2(60); HOUR_GAP NUMBER(5,2); WHEN_REQUIRED CHAR(1)!; ALTERNATE_DAYS CHAR(1)!; PRINT_ON_LABEL CHAR(1); TAPERING_ALLOWED CHAR(1); COMPLEX_FREQUENCY VARCHAR2(1); SHOW_FREQUENCY CHAR(1); DAY_GAP NUMBER(5,2); ALLOWED_IN_OPAT CHAR(1)

fk: (FREQUENCY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FREQUENCY(FREQUENCY_ID)

## FREQUENCY_DOSE_TIME [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FREQUENCY_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; DOSE_TIME VARCHAR2(5)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); PROVISION_TIME CHAR(1)
fk: (FREQUENCY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.FREQUENCY(FREQUENCY_ID)

cols: SR_NO NUMBER(3); GENERIC_CATEGORY VARCHAR2(2); GENERIC_ID VARCHAR2(8); SHOW_IN_HISTORY VARCHAR2(1) «Show in History flag»; ADULT_PEADS VARCHAR2(1) «A -> Adults, P -> Peads»; SHOW_DISPENSING_DOSE VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (SR_NO, GENERIC_CATEGORY) → DEFINITIONS.GENERIC_CATEGORY_MASTER(SR_NO, GENERIC_CATEGORY)

## GENERIC_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: GENERIC_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## GLOBAL_VARIABLES [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains definitions of Global variable those are mainatained at Application level This setup replicates variable values from context data to Db Global variables and Application Global variables
cols: VARIABLE_ID NUMBER* «Store system generated variable ID»; VARIABLE_NAME VARCHAR2(30)! «Store variable name that will be used by HIS as Global Variable»; DEFAULT_VALUE VARCHAR2(300) «Store default constant value of variable»; INITIALIZE_FROM VARCHAR2(3000) «Store function name that can be used to initialize value of global variable»; CHANGEABLE CHAR(1) «Store N or Y to check whether this variable value can be changed within a session of HIS»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000) «Store user remarks for specified global variable»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Active state of Variable Y/N»; DATA_SOURCE CHAR(1) «This column contains flag information to know data source of this global variable (F=Front-End, C=Context, Q=Query)»; ORDER_BY VARCHAR2(4) «This coolumn contains order by position to display variable list is sequence»; MANDATORY CHAR(1) «This column contains information that this global variable is mondatory for login time»
uk: (VARIABLE_NAME)
check: (CHANGEABLE IN ('N','Y'))
check: (VARIABLE_NAME = UPPER(VARIABLE_NAME))
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (DATA_SOURCE in ('F','C','Q'))

check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y', 'N'))

check: (SMS IN ('Y','N'))

## LIST_ITEM_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]

fk: (LOV_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LIST_ITEM_DETAIL(LOV_ID)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y', 'N'))

cols: LOCATION_ID NUMBER*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(300)!
uk: (DESCRIPTION)

## LOCATION_PACKAGE_TYPE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to link the Package Type with the Location
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Location Ref»; PACKAGE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Package Type Ref»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)
fk: (PACKAGE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGE_TYPE(PACKAGE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## PATIENT_CATEGORY [+audit] [+ml] — This Table will be used to define the Patient Categories, This table will be considered as System Constant
cols: PATIENT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(8)* «This column will represents the Patient Category, Counter will start from 001 and increase with 1»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(120)! «Name of the Patient Category e.g. Executive, Labour etc.»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
uk: (DESCRIPTION)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## LOCATION_PATIENT_CATEGORY [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to link the Patient Category with the Location
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column will represents the Location within an organization»; PATIENT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(8)* «This column will represents the Patient Category»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)
fk: (PATIENT_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_CATEGORY(PATIENT_CATEGORY_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## LOCATION_RADIOLOGY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Radiology Location ID Reference to definitions.location»; LOCATION_DESC VARCHAR2(60) «Option Description of Location to Display»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Active status of Locaiton (Y=Active,N=Inactive)»; CONTINGENCY_FLAG CHAR(1)! «Coningency status of Locaiton (Y=Active,N=Inactive)»; CONTINGENCY_REMARKS VARCHAR2(500) «Coningency remarks»; REPORTS_ONLINE CHAR(1)! «Report of this Locaiton are available at Website( Y=Yes, N=No)»; SEND_REPORTS CHAR(1)! «Send reports to location/patient directly as PDF File»; SEND_REPORTS_EMAIL VARCHAR2(200) «Send reports to location/patient Email Address»; SEND_ALERTS CHAR(1)! «Send Alerts to location»; SEND_ALERTS_EMAIL VARCHAR2(200) «Send Alerts to location»
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (CONTINGENCY_FLAG in ('Y','N'))
check: (REPORTS_ONLINE in ('Y','N'))
check: (SEND_REPORTS in ('Y','N'))
check: (SEND_ALERTS in ('Y','N'))

## LOCATION_SCHEMAS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains unique Location ID for Radiology service facility»; SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Module/Schema reference ID (eg S25=Pathology,S12=Radiology )»; LOCATION_DESC VARCHAR2(150) «This column contains Location Description/Name»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(15) «This column contains Short Description»; PHONE_NO VARCHAR2(40); FAX_NO VARCHAR2(40); EMAIL VARCHAR2(100) «This column contains Email Address for Radiology Location»; START_DATE DATE «Make active for transaction from date»; CLOSE_DATE DATE «End date limit to keep active for transaction»; SHOW_IN_REPORTS CHAR(1)! «Flag Information (Y=Show in reports menu,N=Do not show)»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(3)! «This column contains order by information to display locations»
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)

## LOCATION_WISE_BRAND [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; BRAND_ID VARCHAR2(12)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## LOCATION_WISE_CC_RIDERS [+audit]
cols: DUTY_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; CC_RIDER_MRNO VARCHAR2(14)*; REMARKS VARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## LOCATION_WISE_CPT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Active status flag Y=Yes,N=No»; MAX_LIMIT NUMBER(6) «Location wise CPT maximum ordering limit»; ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3) «New Column is added by Billing Team , Purpose of this Column is to get CPT Price Organization Wise»; PRICE NUMBER(12,2); CONSENT_EVENT_ID NUMBER(3) «Consent required before this event»; CONSENT_VALIDITY_DAYS NUMBER(3) «Consent validity in no. of days»; DURATION_RESTRICTED CHAR(1) «Use for duration restricted authorization»; LAST_ORDER_VALUE_INHOUR NUMBER(5)!; PATIENT_TYPE_RESTRICTION VARCHAR2(1) «Use for Patient type Restrictions»; ORDER_STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(3) «Use for Location wise Order Status»; PRICE_ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column will be used to activate/ de-activate price of a cpt at specific location.»
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## LOCATION_WISE_CPT_DEPT_SECT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; SECTION_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; CLINICAL_INFORMATION_REQUIRED VARCHAR2(1) «Clinical information required 'Y' mean clinical information is mandatory to Order CPT.»; PERFORMER_REQUIRED VARCHAR2(1) «Performer Required 'Y' mean performer selection is mandatory to Order CPT.»
fk: (DEPARTMENT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT(DEPARTMENT_ID) [disabled]
fk: (DEPARTMENT_ID, SECTION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT_SECTION(DEPARTMENT_ID, SECTION_ID) [disabled]
uk: (LOCATION_ID, CPT_ID, DEPARTMENT_ID)

## LOCATION_WISE_CPT_PRICE_UPDATE
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; EFFECTIVE_DATE DATE*; NEW_PRICE NUMBER!; PRICE_ACTIVE CHAR(1); TRANS_DATE DATE; STATUS CHAR(1) «"P" for Pending and "C" for Completed»; STATUS_UPDATE_DATE DATE

## LOCATION_WISE_DBSERVICES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; SERVICE_ID VARCHAR2(4)!; STANDBY_SERVICE_ID VARCHAR2(4)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
fk: (SERVICE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DB_SERVICES(SERVICE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (STANDBY_SERVICE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DB_SERVICES(SERVICE_ID) [disabled]

## LOCATION_WISE_DIMENSIONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; NAME VARCHAR2(500); ADDRESS VARCHAR2(1000); LATITUDE VARCHAR2(50)!; LONGITUDE VARCHAR2(50)!; PLUS_CODE VARCHAR2(250)
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)

## LOCATION_WISE_GENERIC [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; GENERIC_ID VARCHAR2(8)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## LOCATION_WISE_MONTHS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; MONTH CHAR(6)*; MON_START_DATE DATE!; MON_END_DATE DATE!; FLAG VARCHAR2(1); MONTH_CLOSED VARCHAR2(1); NEXT_MONTH VARCHAR2(1); CURRENT_MONTH VARCHAR2(1); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(15); PAY_FLAG CHAR(1); PAY_PROCESS CHAR(1); PAY_CALC_DATE DATE; PAY_POST_DATE DATE; PAY_START_DATE DATE; PAY_END_DATE DATE; LEAVE_BALANCE_CALC CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000)

## LOCATION_WISE_OBJECT_SCHEDULE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)*; DAY_ID NUMBER(1)*; RESTRICTED_FROM DATE «use only time factor»; RESTRICTED_UPTO DATE «use only time factor»; ACTIVE CHAR(1); STANDBY_RESTRICTED_FROM DATE «use only time factor»; STANDBY_RESTRICTED_TO DATE «use only time factor»
fk: (DAY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DAY(DAY_ID) [disabled]

## PATIENT_TYPE_CONSTANT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CONSTANT_ID NUMBER(2)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## LOCATION_WISE_PATIENT_TYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); PREFIX_LOCTION_ID VARCHAR2(3); DEFAULT_PATIENT_TYPE CHAR(1); CONSTANT_ID NUMBER(2); COUNTER NUMBER(6); PREFIX VARCHAR2(3); INTER_CONVERSION NUMBER(3); YEAR_WISE_COUNTER CHAR(1)!; DEFAULT_LOCATION CHAR(1); CONTRACT_AT_RUNTIME CHAR(1)! «Values of this column must be Y and N. If vlaues of this column will be "N" then system will not attach the contracts at runtime, otherwise Invoice Procedure will attach the contracts at runtime and generate the invoice accordingly»; CAFE_FACILITY_ALLOWED CHAR(1)! «Values of this column must be Y or N, Y means Employee of this patient Type is allowed to buy food items from Cafe on Credit»; EMP_SALARY_CHECK CHAR(1)! «It is a Common Business Rule that We will allow the employees who are on our payroll to buy Food Items from Cafe but there is some exception When a doctor is not on payroll then no need to check the Salary of that patient, in such cases Values of this column will be N»; CREDIT_LIMIT_SAL_PERCENT NUMBER(3)! «How much an employee can avail of his/her salary as a Credit, This column is dependant of above column (EMP_SALARY_CHECK), if values of the above column will be Y then this column is applicable»; PROVIDENT_FUND_CHECK CHAR(1)! «If value of this column is Y then This Employee must be member of Provident Fund otherwise he/she is not eligible for Credit , if value of this column is N then System will not check the Provident Fund Membership»; CAFE_FACILITY_ALLOW_PROBATION CHAR(1)! «This column will be used to stop or allow the Employee for Cafe Facilities during Probation»; PI_PAYMENT_GL_PAYROLL CHAR(1)! «This column will be used for handling of Performer Practice Income through Payroll or GL, Practice Income Process will make decision on based of this flag and add payroll.allowance_deduction_detail table in case of G and Generate the GL voucher in case of G»; CAFE_DEDUCTION_GL_PAYROLL CHAR(1)! «This column will be used to define the setup whether Cafe Deduction of this Patient Type will be made through Payroll or GL. Values must be P and G, P for Payroll and G for GL»; REG_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED CHAR(1) «PURPOSE: Y -> REGISTRATION REQUIRED FOR PATIENT, N-> NO REGISTRATION REQUIRED FOR PATIENT.»; FOOD_COUPON_ALLOW CHAR(1)! «Values of this Column will be Y or N, In case of Y system will generate the Food Coupon from invoice if other conditions meets, If values of this column is N then System will not generate the Food Coupon»; REG_VERIFICATION_REQUIED CHAR(1); INVOICE_RETURN_PREFIX CHAR(1) «This column will be used to define the Invoice\ Return Prefix If New Invoice\ Return No. Counter is required for this Patient Type, If Value of this column is not null then System will generate the Separate Invoice Counte for This patient Type and Add the Value of this column in Invoice\ Return N...»; ALLOW_BBK_PRODUCT_ORDERING CHAR(1); OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11) «This Column will be used to save object code of form or report if any decision required based on patient type and location»; URGENT CHAR(1) «This column contains info about urgent applicable for Radiology and Nuclear Medicine if its value is Y»; PATIENT_EDUCATION CHAR(1) «This column contains info about patient education applicable for physician Notes»; REPORT_VIEWED CHAR(1) «This column contains info about patient report viewed by any doctor. Y=Viewed , N=Not Viewed»; HIDE_TIME_GIVEN_PT_WO CHAR(1) «This column contains flag Y=Yes , N=Not Time given to Patient need to be Hide or not»; ADMISSION_ALLOWED CHAR(1)! «This column will be used to restrict/ allow the admission on specific location for specific patient type, by default Admission is restricted for all patient types, user has to allow manually»; CONTARCT_DAYS_FOR_CAFE NUMBER; TAX_EXEMPTION CHAR(1)
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)
fk: (PATIENT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE(PATIENT_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (CONSTANT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE_CONSTANT(CONSTANT_ID) [disabled]
check: (YEAR_WISE_COUNTER in ('Y','N'))
check: (CREDIT_LIMIT_SAL_PERCENT BETWEEN 0 AND 100)
check: (CAFE_FACILITY_ALLOWED IN ('Y','N'))
check: (EMP_SALARY_CHECK IN ('Y','N'))
check: (PROVIDENT_FUND_CHECK IN ('Y','N'))
check: (PI_PAYMENT_GL_PAYROLL IN ('P','G','N'))
check: (CAFE_DEDUCTION_GL_PAYROLL IN ('P','G'))
check: (CONTRACT_AT_RUNTIME IN ('N','Y'))

fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)
fk: (RPT_HF_SETUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.RPT_HF_SETUP(RPT_HF_SETUP_ID) [disabled]

## LOC_UNIT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; SUCCESS VARCHAR2(1); TRANS_DATTM DATE

## LOC_WISE_CONSENT_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SR_NO NUMBER*; CONSENT_FORM_TYPE VARCHAR2(2) «T -> Transfusion of Blood Products A -> Anaesthesia & Sedation Services O -> Operation / Procedure C -> Chemotherapy Administration R -> Radionuclide Therapy P -> Therapeutic Apheresis PP -> Plasmapheresis (Plasma Exchange) PT -> Therapeutic Plateletpheresis PL -> Leukapheresis»; QUEUE_GENERATION VARCHAR2(1) «This flag will return either generate consent queues org loc wise or not, if flag reflects Y mean generate queues for N no queue will be generated and consent will be complete on sign»; DIGITALLY_SIGN VARCHAR2(1) «Y => DIGITALLY SIGN REQUIRED , N => NOT REQUIRED»
fk: (CONSENT_FORM_TYPE) → DEFINITIONS.CONSENT_TYPE(CONSENT_FORM_TYPE)
uk: (CONSENT_FORM_TYPE, LOC_ID)

## LOC_WISE_PAT_TYPE_RESTRICTION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); PATIENT_TYPE_RESTRICTION VARCHAR2(1) «Use for Patient type Restrictions»

## LOC_WISE_PHARMACY_STORE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; PHYSICAL_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; STORE_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

cols: SPECIALTY_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; SERVICE_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## METHOD [+audit] [+ml]
cols: METHOD VARCHAR2(20)* «WEBLOGIC/API Name»
check: (METHOD = UPPER(METHOD))

## MI [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CLIENT_ID VARCHAR2(10)!; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)!; PRICE NUMBER(12,2)!

## MICRO_SERVICES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERVICE_ID VARCHAR2(9)*; SERVICE_NAME VARCHAR2(100); SERVICE_DESC VARCHAR2(100); LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); ZONE_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); PARENT_LOCATION VARCHAR2(3); CURRENT_LOCATION CHAR(1)!; APPLICATION_ID VARCHAR2(50); SERVICE_URL_PUBLIC VARCHAR2(1000); SERVICE_URL_LOCAL VARCHAR2(1000); IS_URL_PUBLIC CHAR(1)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; ORDER_BY NUMBER

## MLOG$_DEPARTMENT [+audit] [+ml] — snapshot log for master table DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT
cols: M_ROW$$ VARCHAR2(255); DMLTYPE$$ VARCHAR2(1); OLD_NEW$$ VARCHAR2(1); CHANGE_VECTOR$$ RAW(255); XID$$ NUMBER

## MLOG$_GL_DEPARTMENTS [+audit] [+ml] — snapshot log for master table DEFINITIONS.GL_DEPARTMENTS
cols: M_ROW$$ VARCHAR2(255); DMLTYPE$$ VARCHAR2(1); OLD_NEW$$ VARCHAR2(1); CHANGE_VECTOR$$ RAW(255); XID$$ NUMBER

## MLOG$_GL_DEPT_SERVICES [+audit] [+ml] — snapshot log for master table DEFINITIONS.GL_DEPT_SERVICES
cols: M_ROW$$ VARCHAR2(255); DMLTYPE$$ VARCHAR2(1); OLD_NEW$$ VARCHAR2(1); CHANGE_VECTOR$$ RAW(255); XID$$ NUMBER

## MLOG$_UNIT [+audit] [+ml] — snapshot log for master table DEFINITIONS.UNIT
cols: M_ROW$$ VARCHAR2(255); SNAPTIME$$ DATE; DMLTYPE$$ VARCHAR2(1); OLD_NEW$$ VARCHAR2(1); CHANGE_VECTOR$$ RAW(255); XID$$ NUMBER

## MOBILE_CODES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MOBILE_CODE_NO VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255)
check: (DESCRIPTION IN ('MOBILINK','WARID','INSTAPHONE','TELENOR','ZONG','UFONE','SCOM','ETISALAT','OTHERS'))

## MOBILITY_STATUS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MOBILITY_STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; MOBILITY_STATUS CHAR(1)!; MOBILITY_DESC VARCHAR2(200)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; ENTERED_BY VARCHAR2(14); ENTERED_DATE DATE; ENTERED_TERMINAL VARCHAR2(30); LOC_ID VARCHAR2(3)*
uk: (MOBILITY_DESC, LOC_ID)
uk: (MOBILITY_STATUS, LOC_ID)

## MODIFY_REASONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MODIFY_REASON_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## MODULE_REPORT_NAME [+audit] [+ml]
cols: REPORT_NAME VARCHAR2(30); MODULE_NAME VARCHAR2(60); PIC_FLAG CHAR(1); ACTIVE CHAR(1); DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7); PDF_REPORT_NAME VARCHAR2(30); PDF_REPORT_SERVER VARCHAR2(30); PDF_REPORT_PATH VARCHAR2(60); RDF_REPORT_SERVER VARCHAR2(30); RDF_REPORT_PATH VARCHAR2(60); JPEG_REPORT_SERVER VARCHAR2(30); JPEG_REPORT_PATH VARCHAR2(30); RDF_REPORT_NAME VARCHAR2(200); PDF_DESNAME VARCHAR2(200); REPORT_NAME_ID NUMBER(3)* «Primary Key»

## MONTHS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: START_DATE DATE* «Store attendance month start date»; END_DATE DATE* «Store attendance month end date»; FLAG VARCHAR2(1) «This column can contain 'Y' or 'N'. It's value will be 'Y' only for current month»; MONTH_CLOSED VARCHAR2(1) «This column will save whether month is closed or not for payroll processing»; NEXT_MONTH VARCHAR2(1) «This column will be 'Y' for next month. Only one month can be selected as 'Y'»; CURRENT_MONTH VARCHAR2(1) «This column will be 'Y' for current month. Only one month can be selected as 'Y'»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(15) «Store month short description as Jan, 2010»; PAY_FLAG CHAR(1); PAY_GL_VOUCHER CHAR(1); PAY_PF_VOUCHER CHAR(1); PAY_PROCESS CHAR(1) «Store whether payroll is processed for specified month or not»; PAY_CALC_DATE DATE «Store date on which payroll is executed for specified month»; PAY_POST_DATE DATE; PAY_START_DATE DATE «Store month start date for payroll»; PAY_END_DATE DATE «Store month end date for payroll»; MONTH CHAR(6) «Store month Id as '012010' for Jan 2010»; LEAVE_BALANCE_CALC CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000) «To save comments when monthly regular process is executed for payroll to avoid any data manipulation on dependant table»; CISCO_PROCESS_DATE DATE; CISCO_POST_DATE DATE
check: (MONTH_CLOSED IN ('N','Y'))
check: (NEXT_MONTH IN ('N','Y'))
check: (CURRENT_MONTH IN ('N','Y'))
check: (LEAVE_BALANCE_CALC IN ('Y','N'))

## MONTH_RFID_DATA_TRANSFER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: START_DATE DATE*; END_DATE DATE*; TRANSFER_ID NUMBER(1)*; RFID_DATA_TRANSFERRED CHAR(1); RFID_DATA_COMMENTS VARCHAR2(1000); TRANSFERED_BY VARCHAR2(14); TRANSFER_DATE DATE
fk: (START_DATE, END_DATE) → DEFINITIONS.MONTHS(START_DATE, END_DATE)

## MORPHOLOGY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MORPH_CODE VARCHAR2(7)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(200); ICD_O_3_CODE CHAR(4); ICD_O_3_GRADE CHAR(1); ACTIVE CHAR(1); FU_FOR_LIFE CHAR(1) «Y mean for life time follow up treatment»; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)!

## MOVEMENT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MOVEMENT_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
uk: (DESCRIPTION)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))

## MRN_TRAN_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MRN_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(1)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(50)!; TRANS_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)!; ORDER_BY NUMBER(3); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*

## M_CODES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: M_CODE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Snomed M-Code ID»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column contains the Active Status. (Y=ACTIVE, N=Not-Active)»

## NC_ACTION_TAKEN [+audit]
cols: ACTION_TAKEN_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; ACTION_TAKEN_DESC VARCHAR2(500)!; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)!; LOC_ID VARCHAR2(3); ZON_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORG_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## NC_CLINICAL_AREA [+audit]
cols: CLINICAL_AREA_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; CLINICAL_AREA_DESC VARCHAR2(500)!; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)!; LOC_ID VARCHAR2(3); ZON_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORG_ID VARCHAR2(3); COMPLIANCE_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)! «OCA for Open Chart Audit CCA for Close Chart Audit»; CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(4)

## NC_QUESTION_CATEGORY [+audit]
cols: CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; CATEGORY_DESC VARCHAR2(500)!; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)!; LOC_ID VARCHAR2(3); ZON_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORG_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## NC_QUESTION_SETUP [+audit]
cols: QUESTION_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; QUESTION_DESC VARCHAR2(500)!; CLINICAL_AREA_ID VARCHAR2(4)!; CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(4)!; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)!; LOC_ID VARCHAR2(3); ZON_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORG_ID VARCHAR2(3); ORDER_BY NUMBER(6)

## NGINX_CONFIGURATION
cols: APP_NAME VARCHAR2(100)*; APP_URL VARCHAR2(255); APP_VERSION VARCHAR2(20); ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; CONFIG_TYPE CHAR(1)!; NGNIX_CONFIG CLOB; PORT NUMBER(4); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000); APP_ID NUMBER
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y', 'N'))
check: (CONFIG_TYPE IN ('D', 'S'))

## NHSN_SETUP_RATIO_M [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100)

## NHSN_SETUP_RATIO_D [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ID VARCHAR2(3)*; INFECTION_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; RATIO_VALUE NUMBER(3,2); ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (ID) → DEFINITIONS.NHSN_SETUP_RATIO_M(ID)

## NOTES_ALERT_PARAM [+audit] [+ml]
cols: NOTES_ID NUMBER(3)*; PARAM_ID NUMBER(3)*; TEXT VARCHAR2(30); REPLACE_WITH_PARAM VARCHAR2(30); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## NOTES_TEMPLATE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: NOTES_ID NUMBER(3)*; NOTES_DESC VARCHAR2(60); NOTES_TEXT VARCHAR2(1000); ACTIVE CHAR(1); LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)

## NOTE_TEMPLATE_ALERTS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TEMPLATE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; POPUP_ALERT_ID NUMBER(5)*; ALERT_TYPE_ID NUMBER(5)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (POPUP_ALERT_ID, ALERT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CLINICAL_POPUP_ALERTS(POPUP_ALERT_ID, ALERT_TYPE_ID) [disabled]

## NOTE_TEMPLATE_CPT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TEMPLATE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; PARAMETER_ID VARCHAR2(6); CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; MAKE_ORDER CHAR(1) «Y mean make order master/cpt against specified CPT ID»; IPD_VISIT_CHARGE CHAR(1) «Y mean IPD visit charging entery make into system against specified CPT ID»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## NOTE_TEMPLATE_REPORT_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SETUP_ID NUMBER(6)*; TEMPLATE_ID VARCHAR2(6)!; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)!; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)!; REMARKS VARCHAR2(500)
uk: (TEMPLATE_ID, OBJECT_CODE, LOC_ID)

## NOTE_USERS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: USER_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(200); ACTIVE CHAR(1); PARENT_USER_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)*

## NURSING_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SETUP_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

cols: SETUP_ID VARCHAR2(3); DEFINITION_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (SETUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.NURSING_SETUP(SETUP_ID) [disabled]

## NURSING_SETUP_DEFINITION_DTL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SETUP_ID VARCHAR2(3); DEFINITION_ID VARCHAR2(6); CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(9)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); RANGE_FROM NUMBER(5,2); RANGE_TO NUMBER(5,2); CATEGORY_TYPE CHAR(1); ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (DEFINITION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.NURSING_SETUP_DEFINITION(DEFINITION_ID) [disabled]

cols: P_OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)*; C_OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)*; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(500)
fk: (P_OBJECT_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE)
fk: (C_OBJECT_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE) [disabled]

## OBJECTS_BACKUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3); OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3); OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5); NAME VARCHAR2(1000)!; PATH_ID VARCHAR2(5); SUPERVISED_BY VARCHAR2(14); OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11); DISPLAY_NAME VARCHAR2(60); INITIAL_SCREEN CHAR(1); DEPLOYMENT_DATE DATE; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(2000); OTHER_TITLE_TXT VARCHAR2(30); OTHER_COMMENTS_TXT VARCHAR2(2000); DEPLOYED_YN CHAR(1); TERMINAL_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(15); PURPOSE VARCHAR2(2500); DEV_TOOL VARCHAR2(4)!; CREATE_SESSION CHAR(1)!; IS_JSP CHAR(1); OBSOLETED_OBJECT CHAR(1)!; OBSOLETED_BY VARCHAR2(14); RETIRED_6I CHAR(1); RESTRICTED CHAR(1)!; EMAIL_SEND CHAR(1)!; STOP_AUTO_COMPILE CHAR(1); PARAM_FORM_REQ CHAR(1); OBJECT_SKIP_SECURITY CHAR(1); WIN_X_POS NUMBER(5,2); WIN_Y_POS NUMBER(5,2); WIN_WIDTH NUMBER(5,2); WIN_HEIGHT NUMBER(5,2); REPORT_ID NUMBER(4); REPORT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(4); OBJECT_NATURE CHAR(1); RUN_FROM_SERVER VARCHAR2(4); SERVER_TYPE CHAR(1); IS_CONFIDENTIAL_OBJ CHAR(1); IS_REPORTING_FORM CHAR(1); OBJECT_OPEN_MAX_TIME NUMBER; MDI_FORM CHAR(1); OBJECT_URL VARCHAR2(256); OBJECT_URL_USER VARCHAR2(50); OBJECT_URL_PASSWORD VARCHAR2(50); OBJECT_BROWSER_EXE_NAME VARCHAR2(250); SPACE_REQ_AFTER_EXE_NAME CHAR(1); OBJECT_PAGE VARCHAR2(60); PAGE_ID NUMBER; APP_ID NUMBER; PLATFORM CHAR(1); DISTRIBUTED_MODE CHAR(2)
