cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11); OBJECT_IMAGE BLOB; IMAGE_NAME VARCHAR2(200)

## OBJECTS_TEMP [+audit]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3); OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3); OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5); NAME VARCHAR2(1000)!; PATH_ID VARCHAR2(5); SUPERVISED_BY VARCHAR2(14); OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11); DISPLAY_NAME VARCHAR2(60); INITIAL_SCREEN CHAR(1); DEPLOYMENT_DATE DATE; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(2000); OTHER_TITLE_TXT VARCHAR2(30); OTHER_COMMENTS_TXT VARCHAR2(2000); DEPLOYED_YN CHAR(1); TERMINAL_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(15); PURPOSE VARCHAR2(2500); DEV_TOOL VARCHAR2(4)!; CREATE_SESSION CHAR(1)!; IS_JSP CHAR(1); OBSOLETED_OBJECT CHAR(1)!; OBSOLETED_BY VARCHAR2(14); RETIRED_6I CHAR(1); RESTRICTED CHAR(1)!; EMAIL_SEND CHAR(1)!; STOP_AUTO_COMPILE CHAR(1); PARAM_FORM_REQ CHAR(1); OBJECT_SKIP_SECURITY CHAR(1); WIN_X_POS NUMBER(5,2); WIN_Y_POS NUMBER(5,2); WIN_WIDTH NUMBER(5,2); WIN_HEIGHT NUMBER(5,2); REPORT_ID NUMBER(4); REPORT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(4); OBJECT_NATURE CHAR(1); RUN_FROM_SERVER VARCHAR2(4); SERVER_TYPE CHAR(1); IS_CONFIDENTIAL_OBJ CHAR(1); IS_REPORTING_FORM CHAR(1); OBJECT_OPEN_MAX_TIME NUMBER; MDI_FORM CHAR(1); OBJECT_URL VARCHAR2(256); OBJECT_URL_USER VARCHAR2(50); OBJECT_URL_PASSWORD VARCHAR2(50); OBJECT_BROWSER VARCHAR2(250); WS_SYNC_DATE DATE

## OBJECT_BLOCK [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; BLOCK_NAME VARCHAR2(40); BLOCK_ID NUMBER*
fk: (SCHEMA_ID, OBJECT_TYPE_ID, OBJECT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(SCHEMA_ID, OBJECT_TYPE_ID, OBJECT_ID)

## OBJECT_BUTTON [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; BTTN_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; BTTN_NAME VARCHAR2(100)

## OBJECT_CALL [+audit] [+ml] — Setup table contains information about organization , location base calling objects.
cols: BASE_OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)! «This column contains info about parrent object code»; CHILD_OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)! «This column contains info about child object code»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contains info about status(Y = Active, N = In Active)»
uk: (BASE_OBJECT_CODE, ORG_ID, LOC_ID)

## OBJECT_DEVELOPER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; DEVELOPER_ID VARCHAR2(14)*; EXPECTED_START_DATE DATE; EXPECTED_END_DATE DATE; ACTUAL_END_DATE DATE
fk: (SCHEMA_ID, OBJECT_TYPE_ID, OBJECT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(SCHEMA_ID, OBJECT_TYPE_ID, OBJECT_ID)

## OBJECT_FROM_LIVE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3); OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3); OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5); NAME VARCHAR2(1000)!; PATH_ID VARCHAR2(5); SUPERVISED_BY VARCHAR2(14); OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11); DISPLAY_NAME VARCHAR2(60); INITIAL_SCREEN CHAR(1); DEPLOYMENT_DATE DATE; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(2000); OTHER_TITLE_TXT VARCHAR2(30); OTHER_COMMENTS_TXT VARCHAR2(2000); DEPLOYED_YN CHAR(1); TERMINAL_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(15); PURPOSE VARCHAR2(2500); DEV_TOOL VARCHAR2(4)!; CREATE_SESSION CHAR(1)!; IS_JSP CHAR(1); OBSOLETED_OBJECT CHAR(1)!; OBSOLETED_BY VARCHAR2(14); RETIRED_6I CHAR(1); RESTRICTED CHAR(1)!; EMAIL_SEND CHAR(1)!; STOP_AUTO_COMPILE CHAR(1); PARAM_FORM_REQ CHAR(1); OBJECT_SKIP_SECURITY CHAR(1); WIN_X_POS NUMBER(5,2); WIN_Y_POS NUMBER(5,2); WIN_WIDTH NUMBER(5,2); WIN_HEIGHT NUMBER(5,2); REPORT_ID NUMBER(4); REPORT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(4); OBJECT_NATURE CHAR(1); RUN_FROM_SERVER VARCHAR2(4); SERVER_TYPE CHAR(1); IS_CONFIDENTIAL_OBJ CHAR(1); IS_REPORTING_FORM CHAR(1); OBJECT_OPEN_MAX_TIME NUMBER; MDI_FORM CHAR(1); OBJECT_URL VARCHAR2(256); OBJECT_URL_USER VARCHAR2(50); OBJECT_URL_PASSWORD VARCHAR2(50); OBJECT_BROWSER_EXE_NAME VARCHAR2(250); SPACE_REQ_AFTER_EXE_NAME CHAR(1); OBJECT_PAGE VARCHAR2(60); PAGE_ID NUMBER; APP_ID NUMBER; PLATFORM CHAR(1); DISTRIBUTED_MODE CHAR(2)

## OBJECT_ITEM [+audit] [+ml] — This Table contains Object Items List that are used in Forms
cols: ITEM_ID NUMBER(6)* «Unique Item ID»; ITEM_TYPE_ID NUMBER(3)! «This column contains Item Type ID (Reference to DEFINITIONS.ITEM_TYPES)»; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)! «This column contains Object Code (Reference to DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS)»; ITEM_NAME VARCHAR2(65)! «This column contains Item Name that is used in Objects as Identifier (BLOCK_NAME.ITEM_NAME)»; ITEM_DESC VARCHAR2(100) «This column contains Descriptive Name of Item that is used in Objects»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contains Flag Information of Status (Y=Active, N=Inactive)»
fk: (ITEM_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ITEM_TYPES(TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (OBJECT_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE)
uk: (OBJECT_CODE, ITEM_NAME)
uk: (ITEM_ID, ITEM_TYPE_ID)
check: (ITEM_NAME = UPPER(ITEM_NAME))
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## OBJECT_ITEM_PROPERTY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ITEM_ID NUMBER(6)*; PROPERTY_ID NUMBER(2)*; VALUE_SOURCE CHAR(1)! «'O' in case of open text, 'D' is denominating that the value is from Dictionary table (Definitions.Terminology), 'X' means that the value is from fixed values table(Definitions.Item_Property_values)»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
fk: (ITEM_ID) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECT_ITEM(ITEM_ID)
fk: (PROPERTY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ITEM_PROPERTY(PROPERTY_ID) [disabled]
fk: (VALUE_SOURCE) → DEFINITIONS.ITEM_PROPERTY_SOURCES(VALUE_SOURCE) [disabled]
check: (VALUE_SOURCE in ('X','D','O'))
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## OBJECT_MAPPING_APEX [+audit] [+ml]
cols: OBJECT_CODE_LEGACY VARCHAR2(11)*; OBJECT_CODE_APEX VARCHAR2(11)*; PLATE_FORM VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE CHAR(1); RIGHTS_COPY_STATUS CHAR(1)
fk: (OBJECT_CODE_LEGACY) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE)

## OBJECT_NOTE_PRINT [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to Add the Different Notes which will be printed on Reports, Notes will be Organization+Location+Object Code Wise
cols: ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Organization for whom Note Setup will be entered»; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Location for whom Note Setup will be entered»; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)* «Object Code on which Notes will be printed»; SR_NO NUMBER(1)* «This column was added because more than one Note can be entered on the Same Object»; TRANS_DATE DATE! «Date When Transaction will be added»; NOTE VARCHAR2(1000)! «Note which will be printed on Report»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction, Values will be Y or N»
fk: (ORGANIZATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORGANIZATION(ORGANIZATION_ID)
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID) [disabled]
fk: (OBJECT_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE) [disabled]

## OBJECT_PARAMETERS_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PARAM_ID NUMBER*; PARAM_NAME VARCHAR2(100)*; PARAM_VALUE VARCHAR2(300); PARAM_LENGTH NUMBER(2); PARAM_ORDER_BY NUMBER(2); PARAM_DATA_TYPE VARCHAR2(1) «Specify parameter data type 'N' for number, 'D' for Date & 'C' for character»
fk: (PARAM_ID) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECT_PARAMETERS(PARAM_ID)
check: (PARAM_DATA_TYPE IN ('N','C','D'))

## OBJECT_PARAMETERS_DETAIL_COPY_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PARAM_ID NUMBER!; PARAM_NAME VARCHAR2(100)!; PARAM_VALUE VARCHAR2(300); PARAM_LENGTH NUMBER(2); PARAM_ORDER_BY NUMBER(2); PARAM_DATA_TYPE VARCHAR2(1)

## OBJECT_PARAMETERS_SOURCE [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains parent/calling object wise front end value source of parameters
cols: PARAM_ID NUMBER(7)* «This column referes to parent table DEFINITIONS.OBJECT_PARAMETERS (PK)»; CALLING_OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)* «This column contains parent calling obejct code (PK)»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contains flag inforamtion of active status Y=Active, N=Inactive»; CHECK_DEFAULT CHAR(1)! «This column contains flag inforamtion of default value application in case of null values»; FRONTEND_SOURCE VARCHAR2(100) «This column contains string to access front end values source»; DEFAULT_VALUE VARCHAR2(100) «This column contains default value if frontend value does not defined»
fk: (PARAM_ID) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECT_PARAMETERS(PARAM_ID)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (CHECK_DEFAULT in ('Y','N'))

## OBJECT_PARAMETERS_SOURCE_COPY_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PARAM_ID NUMBER(7)!; CALLING_OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; CHECK_DEFAULT CHAR(1)!; FRONTEND_SOURCE VARCHAR2(100); DEFAULT_VALUE VARCHAR2(100)

## OBJECT_PATIENT_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERIAL_NO NUMBER*; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)!; PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)!; REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000)
fk: (OBJECT_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE)
fk: (PATIENT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE(PATIENT_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
uk: (OBJECT_CODE, PATIENT_TYPE_ID)

## OBJECT_PREREQUISITES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERIAL_NO NUMBER(3)* «Serial No for the each prerequisit against Object Code»; SOURCE_NAME VARCHAR2(500)! «Function Name»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100) «Detail description of prerequisit»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «Y for active, N for not active»; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11)* «Refer from DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS table»; HELP_TEXT VARCHAR2(1000) «Help text for MIS Helpline»
fk: (OBJECT_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.OBJECTS(OBJECT_CODE)

## OBJECT_RULES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; OBJECT_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; RULE_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(1000)*; RULE_DATE DATE; CR_ID VARCHAR2(100); CR_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(1000); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## PHY_NOTES_PLAN [+audit]
cols: PLAN_ID NUMBER*; NAME VARCHAR2(500); REMARKS VARCHAR2(1500); ACTIVE CHAR(1); ORDER_BY NUMBER

## OBJECT_WISE_NOTES_PLAN [+audit]
cols: OBJECT_WISE_PLAN_ID NUMBER*; PLAN_ID NUMBER; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(500); REMARKS VARCHAR2(1500); ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (PLAN_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PHY_NOTES_PLAN(PLAN_ID)

## OFFICE_CITIES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)*; STATE_ID NUMBER(4)*; DISTRICT_ID NUMBER(4)*; TEHSIL_ID NUMBER(4)*; NAME VARCHAR2(60); OFFICE_ID NUMBER(3)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (COUNTRY_ID, STATE_ID, DISTRICT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DISTRICT(COUNTRY_ID, STATE_ID, DISTRICT_ID)

## OLD_DATA_COUNTER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TRANSACTION_TYPE_ID CHAR(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); COUNTER NUMBER(7); TRN_YEAR CHAR(2)

## OLD_DATA_COUNTER_HUSSAIN_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TRANSACTION_TYPE_ID CHAR(3); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); COUNTER NUMBER(7); TRN_YEAR CHAR(2)

## ONLINE_SURVEY_ANSWERS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ANSWER_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; DISPLAY_ORDER NUMBER(3)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))

## ONLINE_SURVEY_MASTER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SURVEY_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500)

## ONLINE_SURVEY_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SURVEY_ID NUMBER(3)*; START_DATE DATE*; END_DATE DATE*
fk: (SURVEY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ONLINE_SURVEY_MASTER(SURVEY_ID)

## ONLINE_SURVEY_QUESTIONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: QUESTION_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(1000); ACTIVE CHAR(1)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))

## OPEN_QUESTION_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: QUESTION_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## OPEN_QUESTION_SUBTYPE [+audit] [+ml]

## OPERATORS_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: OPERATOR_TYPE_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(200)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «check in (Y¿,`N¿) Default `Y¿»

## OPERATORS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: OPERATOR_ID NUMBER(5)*; OPERATOR_TYPE_ID NUMBER(3)!; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(200)!; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE CHAR(1) «check in (Y¿,`N¿) Default `Y¿»
fk: (OPERATOR_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.OPERATORS_TYPE(OPERATOR_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
uk: (SHORT_DESC)

## ORDER_LOCATION_CLINIC [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; CLINIC_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); DEFAULT_CLINIC VARCHAR2(1)!; STAT_CLINIC VARCHAR2(1)!; ADMIN_DEPARTMENT VARCHAR2(1); PCHS CHAR(1) «Palliative Care Home Services»

## ORDER_LOCATION_CPT_PRICE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Order Location (Invoice Location CPT Price)
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Unique Id of the Institute»; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Invoice Location/Order Location/Counter for which User wants to change the Price of the CPT»; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «CPT Code for which Price will be added»; PRICE NUMBER(12,2)! «Unit Price which will be charged against CPT from this Invoice Location»; PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*
fk: (LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORDER_LOCATION(LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID) [disabled]
check: (PRICE>0)

## ORDER_LOCATION_RECEPTION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; RECEPTION_NAME VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE CHAR(1); RECEPTION_ID NUMBER(10)*; REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000)
fk: (LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORDER_LOCATION(LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID)
uk: (LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID)

## ORDER_LOCATION_ROOM [+audit]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_RECEPTION VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000)

## ORDER_LOCATION_STORE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; STORE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DISPENSING_LOCATION CHAR(1)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); PRIORITY NUMBER; DISPOSABLE VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORDER_LOCATION(LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID)

## ORDER_LOCAT_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; CLINIC_ID VARCHAR2(7); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(20)

## ORDER_LOC_LANGUAGE_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LANGUGAE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; LABEL_DESC NVARCHAR2(500) «Label Desc field will be used to show langugae wise text on Discharge Instruction report on discharge card»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## ORDER_RESTRICTED_CPT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; RESTRICTED_CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## ORGSETUP_GROUPS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: GROUP_ID NUMBER; ORG_SETUP_ID NUMBER*; HIS_ROLE_ID VARCHAR2(10)*

## ORG_FILE_MASTER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORG_FILE_NO CHAR(1)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255)!; LEVELS NUMBER(2)!; NO_OF_DIGITS NUMBER(2)!; LOCKED CHAR(1)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!

## ORG_SETUP_MASTER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORG_SETUP_ID NUMBER(2)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!

## ORG_FILE_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORG_FILE_NO CHAR(1)*; ORG_FILE_DETAIL_ID NUMBER(1)*; WIDTH NUMBER(1)!; ORG_SETUP_ID NUMBER(2)!; KEY_AUTO_MANUAL CHAR(1)!; LEVEL_TYPE CHAR(1)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
fk: (ORG_SETUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORG_SETUP_MASTER(ORG_SETUP_ID) [disabled]
fk: (ORG_FILE_NO) → DEFINITIONS.ORG_FILE_MASTER(ORG_FILE_NO)

## ORG_HF_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; FROM_DATE DATE*; RPT_HF_SETUP_ID CHAR(12)!
fk: (ORGANIZATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORGANIZATION(ORGANIZATION_ID)
fk: (RPT_HF_SETUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.RPT_HF_SETUP(RPT_HF_SETUP_ID) [disabled]

## ORG_SETUP_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ORG_SETUP_ID NUMBER(2)*; ORG_DETAIL_ID NUMBER(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255)!; ADDRESS VARCHAR2(255)!; COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)!; STATE_ID NUMBER(4)!; DISTRICT_ID NUMBER(4)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; ORG_UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(20)
fk: (ORG_SETUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORG_SETUP_MASTER(ORG_SETUP_ID)
fk: (COUNTRY_ID, STATE_ID, DISTRICT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DISTRICT(COUNTRY_ID, STATE_ID, DISTRICT_ID) [disabled]
uk: (ORG_DETAIL_ID)

## ORG_WISE_OBJECT_ITEM_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ITEM_ID NUMBER(6)!; PROPERTY_ID NUMBER(3)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; VALUE VARCHAR2(50)
uk: (ITEM_ID, PROPERTY_ID, ORG_ID)

## OSV_STATUS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: STATUS_ID CHAR(2); OSV_STATUS VARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE CHAR(1); ALERT_DAYS NUMBER; IS_OSV_REQUIRED CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000); IS_SLIP_RECEIVED CHAR(1) «This column will be use for linces expiry slip received purpose»

## OUTSIDE_HOSPITALS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: HOSPITAL_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Unique Hospital ID»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(1000)! «Description of Hospital»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Active Status (Y=Active, N=Inactive)»

## SERVICE_TYPE [+audit] [+ml] — This is a System Constant Table, We shall define the Service Types in this table
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Primary Key, Auto Generated Number»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)! «Name of the Service Type»; SERVICE_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3) «Value of this column will create the differentiate between the Services e.g. CPT, Pharmacy, Supplies, Gym etc.»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Service Type»
fk: (SERVICE_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEF_SERVICE_CATEGORY(SERVICE_CATEGORY_ID) [disabled]
uk: (DESCRIPTION, SERVICE_CATEGORY_ID)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ACTIVE=UPPER(ACTIVE))

## PACKAGE_CPT_INCLUDE_ITEM [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to setup the Supplies already added in Costing of the CPT, This will also handle the Printing of the Package in Detail or Summary like other packages
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Id Ref»; SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column was added to differentiate between This kind of packages and other Packages e.g. Rehab , Radiotherapy, Surgery etc.»; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «CPT Code for Reference to whom Supplies cost is added»; ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(25)* «Supplies/Item whose Cost is added into CPT Price»; QUANTITY NUMBER(5)! «Total Quantity which will be ordered as Free»; TRANS_DATE DATE! «Date when Transaction was added»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(250)! «Remarks if any»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID) [disabled]
fk: (ITEM_ID) → ITEM.ITEM(ITEM_ID) [disabled]

## PACKAGE_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml] — This table is almost parallel of definitions.package (1:1 relation), ADF team was using definitions.packages for C4I, as per ADF Team, if i shall add the column in definitions.packages there application will become invalid that's why Billing Team have created new table
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Ref of Package»; TREATMENT_COVER CHAR(1)* «Values will be O/I/E, O means OPD and I means IPD, E means EAR»; VALIDATION_PERIOD_REQUIRED CHAR(1)! «Values of this column must be Y or N, In case of Y User has to enter the Start and End Date»; START_DATE DATE «Start date of the Package»; END_DATE DATE «End date of the Package»; FROM_TIME VARCHAR2(5) «From time of the Package»; TO_TIME VARCHAR2(5) «To time of the Package»; HIDE_INVOICE_DETAIL CHAR(1)! «Values of this column must be Y or N, Y means Hide the CPT Detail on Invoice Report and Show only Package Name, N means Show CPT Detail»; PRICE_SOURCE CHAR(1); PROCESS_METHOD VARCHAR2(6) «PRE INVOICE, POST INVOICE»; LIFE_SPAN NUMBER(3) «Number of days for which a package can be availed after linked to patient.»; DEFAULT_PKG CHAR(1); DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7); SECTION_ID VARCHAR2(7)
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)
check: (PRICE_SOURCE IN ('P','I'))

## PACKAGE_EXCLUDE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to Add the items as Execluded
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref»; SERIAL_NO NUMBER(3)* «Counter which will reset on New Package»; ITEM_LIST VARCHAR2(500)! «Item's list which is excluded from package»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)

## PACKAGE_FREE_ITEM [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to save the Free Items within Package, This table should be considered as Master level which means If any item is defined in this table then it will be free all the patients to whom This Package is attached
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref»; ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «CPT or Brand or Item which is free due to Package»; TRANS_DATE DATE! «Date when Transaction was made»; NO_OF_ITEM NUMBER(4)! «Total No. of Items allowed Free, Zero means unlimited»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(250) «Remarks if any»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «Status of the Transaction, Y means Active and N means Inactive»; CLIENT_ID VARCHAR2(10) «Which Client contract will be used for Free Items»; CONTRACT_ID VARCHAR2(8) «Which Client contract will be used for Free Items»; CONTRACT_SERIAL_NO NUMBER(3) «Which Client contract will be used for Free Items»; CC_ID VARCHAR2(3) «Which Client contract will be used for Free Items»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)
check: (no_of_item between 0 and 9999)
check: (active in ('Y','N'))

## PACKAGE_FUND [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define funds of a package(to be shared by service types)
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref key»; PACKAGE_FUND_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This Column will represents the fund ID»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(250); AMOUNT NUMBER!; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)
check: (AMOUNT>0)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## PACKAGE_FUND_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define package fund details
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref key»; PACKAGE_FUND_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This Column will represents the Service Type»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID, PACKAGE_FUND_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGE_FUND(PACKAGE_ID, PACKAGE_FUND_ID)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## PACKAGE_INCLUDE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to add the Item Include in Free text, No decision will be made on this table, this is just for Reporting purpose
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref»; SERIAL_NO NUMBER(3)* «Part of the Primary key»; ITEM_LIST VARCHAR2(500)! «Item Name / List in the form of the Free Text»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»

## PACKAGE_ITEM [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to link the Items with the package
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref key»; SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This Column will represents the Service Type»; ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «Item/CPT/Service which will be linked with the package OR which is part of the package»; QUANTITY NUMBER(6)! «Total Quantity which will be ordered when this Package will be used»; PRICE NUMBER(12,2)! «This column will be used to save the Price of the Item (CPT, Drug, Item) within a Package, it can be different from original Table e.g. CBC is 100 in definitions.cpt but it can 110 or 90 in this table»; NO_OF_ITEM_ALLOWED NUMBER! «Total Number of Item Allowed will be entered, if value is zero then it is unlimited»; NO_OF_ITEM_AVAILED NUMBER! «This column will represents the No. of Item Availed, it must be less or equal to NO_OF_ITEM_ALLOWED»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
check: (QUANTITY>=1)
check: (NO_OF_ITEM_ALLOWED>=1)
check: (NO_OF_ITEM_ALLOWED>=NO_OF_ITEM_AVAILED)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## PACKAGE_ITEM_ERRLOG [GTT]
cols: ORA_ERR_NUMBER$ NUMBER; ORA_ERR_MESG$ VARCHAR2(2000); ORA_ERR_ROWID$ ROWID; ORA_ERR_OPTYP$ VARCHAR2(2); ORA_ERR_TAG$ VARCHAR2(2000); PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)*; SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(18)*; WS_SYNC_DATE DATE

## PACKAGE_ITEM_EXTRA_CHARGE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to add the Item list which will be separately charged (If they necessary for the Surgery)
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref»; SERIAL_NO NUMBER(3)* «Counter of the Table which will reset on every new Package»; ITEM_LIST VARCHAR2(500)! «Item List»; ESTIMATED_AMOUNT VARCHAR2(60)! «Minimu to Maximum Cost of the Item List»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)

## PACKAGE_KEY_OBJECT [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to add the key objects of the Package
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref»; SERIAL_NO NUMBER(3)* «Counter of the Table which will reset on every new package»; ITEM_LIST VARCHAR2(500)! «Items list»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)

## PACKAGE_PERFORMER [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to link the Packages with the performer
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Package Ref»; PERFORMER_MRNO VARCHAR2(14)* «User /Performer to whom this Package is linked»; SPECIALITY_ID VARCHAR2(6); ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»; COPIED_FAVOURITE CHAR(1) «This column's value is 'Y' when copied favourites from other user.»; COPIED_FROM VARCHAR2(14) «User Mrno from where favourites is copied.»
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## PACKAGE_SERVICES [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used link the Services Type with the Package
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «This column will represents the Package»; SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This Column will represents the Service Type attached with the Package e.g. Consultation, Radiology etc.»; ALL_SERVICES CHAR(1)! «Value of This column will be Y/N, if Y then All Items of these services are allowed»; TREATMENT_LIMIT_APPLY CHAR(1)! «Values of this column will be Y/N, If value is Y for more than one Services then Amount will be sum up except Medicine/Supplies/Consultation»; TREATMENT_LIMIT_AMOUNT NUMBER(7)! «Total Amount which will be used for this services or sum up with other services»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»; NO_OF_ITEM_ALLOWED NUMBER «Total Number of Item Allowed will be entered, if value is zero or null then it is unlimited»; FREQUENCY CHAR(1) «D FOR DAYS W FOR WEEKS F FORTNIGHTLY M MONTHLY Y YEARLY»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGES(PACKAGE_ID)
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
check: (ALL_SERVICES IN ('Y','N'))
check: (TREATMENT_LIMIT_APPLY IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## ROOM_PAYMENT_CATEGORY [+audit] [+ml]
cols: ROOM_PAYMENT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)!; CPT_ID_SUPPORTED VARCHAR2(18)!; PARENT_PAYMENT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID) [disabled]
uk: (DESCRIPTION)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))

## PACKAGE_STAY [+audit] [+ml] — This table is child of DEFINITIONS.PACKAGE_DETAIL, which contains information about multiple types of stay included in package.
cols: PACKAGE_ID VARCHAR2(10)* «Ref of Package Detail PK»; TREATMENT_COVER CHAR(1)* «Ref of Package Detail PK»; ROOM_PAYMENT_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Ref of Room_Payment_Category»; DAYS NUMBER(2) «Number of days»
fk: (PACKAGE_ID, TREATMENT_COVER) → DEFINITIONS.PACKAGE_DETAIL(PACKAGE_ID, TREATMENT_COVER)
fk: (ROOM_PAYMENT_CATEGORY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ROOM_PAYMENT_CATEGORY(ROOM_PAYMENT_CATEGORY_ID) [disabled]

cols: CAP_CODE NUMBER(9); LOCAL_ID NUMBER(3); FIRST_NAME VARCHAR2(30); LAST_NAME VARCHAR2(30); PARENT_ID VARCHAR2(15)

## PARAMETER_VALUES [+audit] [+ml]

## SYMPTOMS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SYMPTOM_ID NUMBER(4)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); SYMPTOM_TYPE VARCHAR2(2); ACTIVE CHAR(1); ORDER_BY NUMBER

## PARAM_VALUES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PARAMETER_ID NUMBER!; VALUE_ID NUMBER; ACTIVE CHAR(1); ORDER_BY NUMBER; SYMPTOM_ID NUMBER(4); ID NUMBER*
fk: (VALUE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEF_VALUES(VALUE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (PARAMETER_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PARAMETERS(PARAMETER_ID)
fk: (SYMPTOM_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SYMPTOMS(SYMPTOM_ID) [disabled]

cols: PARENT_CLINIC_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

cols: EVENT_ID NUMBER(4)*; EVENT_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; ORDER_BY NUMBER(2)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column contain flag shows active status (Y=active, N=inactive)»; LOG_EVENT CHAR(1) «This column contains Flag Information to log Event (O=Order CPT, D=Module/Detail)»

## PATIENT_DESIGNATION [+audit]
cols: PATIENT_DESIGNATION_ID NUMBER(5)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; STRUCTURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; BASIC_PAY_SCALE VARCHAR2(6)
uk: (DESCRIPTION)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))

## PATIENT_DOC_ATTACHMENT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: MRNO VARCHAR2(14)* «This column contains patient MRNO»; SR_NO NUMBER* «This column contains srno»; DOCUMENT_ID VARCHAR2(13) «This column contains document ID ref lob.document store»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(4000) «This column contains description»; ENTRY_DATE DATE «This column contains entry date»; ATTACHED_BY VARCHAR2(14) «This column contains attached by mrno»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000) «This column contains remarks»; DOC_TYPE_ID NUMBER «This column contains document type ID»
fk: (DOCUMENT_ID) → LOB.DOCUMENTS_STORE(DOCUMENT_ID) [disabled]
fk: (DOC_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ATTACHMENT_DOCUMENT_TYPE(DOC_TYPE_ID) [disabled]

cols: PARAMETER_ID NUMBER(3)* «This col contains parameter id»; PARAMETER_DESC VARCHAR2(100)! «This col contains Parameter Description»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(3) «This col is used to view the records according to this col»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «Y=Active N=Inactive»; ORG_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains Org ID for Multi-location info»; OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11) «This column contains object_code of calling form»

## PATIENT_GROUPS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(5)* «Category/Group ID»; GROUP_DESC VARCHAR2(100)! «Category/Group Description (e.g. Indigent, Diagnostic, Employee etc)»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(3)! «Display order by»; DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7)! «department id foreign key reference DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Y=Active, N=Inactive»; CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; GROUP_CODE VARCHAR2(3) «Unique prefix/identifier for group code»
fk: (DEPARTMENT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT(DEPARTMENT_ID) [disabled]
uk: (CATEGORY_ID, GROUP_DESC)

HIST - will be use for Chemo History form, RDSN - will be use for Resident Verification.»

fk: (ZONE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ZONES(ZONE_ID) [disabled]

## TRANSACTION_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TRANSACTION_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)!; COUNTER NUMBER(7); TRN_YEAR VARCHAR2(2); CLINIC VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)! «Why this column has value A? -- Idrees 21-02-2013»; PHARMACY VARCHAR2(1)!; INVOICE VARCHAR2(1)! «Is this Transaction Type relevant to some kind of invoice? Y mean Yes, N mean No»; INVOICE_TYPE VARCHAR2(1)!; TABLE_REFERENCE VARCHAR2(60); SEQUENCE_NAME VARCHAR2(60); MMS_VOUCHER_TYPE VARCHAR2(5); IN_OUT CHAR(2) «II' Means IPD IN , 'IO' Means IPD Out , 'OI' OPD In, 'OO' Means OPD Out, 'PI' Means Purchase IN , 'PO' Purchase Out»; TRANSACTION_GROUP_ID NUMBER(3) «Transaction group ID referenced from Transaction Group»; MOVE_TYPE_ID NUMBER(3) «used for mapping of move type id with transaction type»; DEFAULT_STOCK_LOCATION VARCHAR2(1) «'Y' := Default stock location of store, 'N' := Transaction order location»
fk: (TRANSACTION_GROUP_ID) → DEFINITIONS.TRANSACTION_GROUP(TRANSACTION_GROUP_ID) [disabled]
fk: (MOVE_TYPE_ID) → MMS.DEF_MOVE_TYPE(MOVE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
check: (INVOICE IN ('Y','N'))
check: (INVOICE_TYPE IN ('Y','N'))
check: (PHARMACY IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N','A'))

## PRIORITY [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains information of priority definitons to perform/teart orderequest on priority basis
cols: PRIORITY_ID CHAR(1)* «This column contains unique Priority ID»; PRIORITY_DESC VARCHAR2(30)! «This column contains Priority full description»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(5)! «This column contains Priority short description»; SHOW_SIGN CHAR(1)! «Flag information, Y=Show warning sign in order queue for priority/urgancy, N=Routine»; BY_DEFAULT CHAR(1)! «Flag information, Y=Default priority if nothing mentioned by user,»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)
check: (PRIORITY_ID between '0' and '9')
check: (SHOW_SIGN in ('Y','N'))
check: (BY_DEFAULT in ('Y','N'))

cols: WORK_FLOW_ID NUMBER(4)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); ACTIVE CHAR(1); FROM_AMOUNT NUMBER(15,4); TO_AMOUNT NUMBER(15,4); BUDGETED CHAR(1); BGT_WORK_FLOW_ID NUMBER(4); APPROVAL_PATH VARCHAR2(500); MEDICAL_ITEM CHAR(1); LC_WORK_FLOW CHAR(1); ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; ZONE_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; SEND_EMAIL CHAR(1) «This column contains flag to use email solution for specific work flow»; WORK_FLOW_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)

fk: (WORK_FLOW_ID) → DEFINITIONS.WORK_FLOW(WORK_FLOW_ID) [disabled]

fk: (EVENT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.EVENT(EVENT_ID) [disabled]

cols: QUALIFICATION_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## QUESTIONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: QUESTION_ID VARCHAR2(9)*; QUESTION_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## QUEUE_TYPES — This table contains information of differnt queues types
cols: QUEUE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(4)* «Unique identifier of queue type»; QUEUE_DESC VARCHAR2(60)! «This column contains description/Label of queue type»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(100) «This column contains open text remarks/other information about quetype»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Thic column contains Flag Information for Active status of queue type Y=ACTIVE,N=INACTIVE»; AUTO_DEL CHAR(1)! «Thic column contains Flag Information for Auto Deletion of record wrt retain limit defined, Y=Auto Delete is ON, N=Off»; RETAIN_LIMIT_TYPE CHAR(1)! «Thic column contains Retain Unit Type X=Not Applicable, D=Days, H=Hours, M=Minutes, (Applicable for Auto Delete =Yes)»; RETAIN_LIMIT NUMBER(5) «Thic column contains Expiry of Limit wrt retain Unit Type (Applicable for Auto Delete =Yes)»; WS_SYNC_DATE DATE
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (AUTO_DEL in ('Y','N'))
check: (RETAIN_LIMIT_TYPE in ('X','D','H','M'))

## RELATION [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains definitions of relations of persons (System Constant Level table)
cols: RELATION_ID VARCHAR2(6)* «Unique Primary Key Relation ID»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60)! «This column contains Name/Description of relation»; DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1)! «This column contains flag of Default to choice relation by choice during registration»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)! «This column contains flag of Active, Inactive state (Y=Active, N=Inactive)»; SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(10) «This column contains Short description/Abbriviations of Relation (e.g. S/O=Son of, W/O=Wife of)»; FOR_QA_CONSENTS CHAR(1)!; EMP_DEPENDANT CHAR(1)! «This column contains flag eaither this relation is of dependant (Y=Dependant, N=Not dependant)»; RELATION_CAT_ID VARCHAR2(3); CHECK_AGE_FOR_MEDICAL CHAR(1) «This flag will check if age limit required to check or not»; AGE_LIMIT NUMBER «This column will store age limit for medical»; RELATION_CATEGORY_ID VARCHAR2(3) «This column contains Relation Category Id Ref. DEFINITIONS.RELATION_CATEGORY»
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (DEFAULTS in ('Y','N'))
check: (EMP_DEPENDANT in ('N','Y'))
check: (FOR_QA_CONSENTS in ('N','Y'))

## REPORT_DESFORMAT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: FORMAT_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains Unique Format ID»; FORMAT_DESC VARCHAR2(50)! «This column contains Name or Description of Report Format»; KEYWORD VARCHAR2(25)! «This column contains Valid Des Format Identifier i.e. understandable for report server engine»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contains Active status of Entry (Y=Active, N=Inactive)»; RP2RRO CHAR(1)! «This column contains Flag Status Y/N either it is used in RP2RRO Library»; DIAGNOSTIC CHAR(1)! «This column contains Flag Status Y/N either this format is used in Diagnostic Reports»
uk: (KEYWORD)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (RP2RRO in ('Y','N'))
check: (DIAGNOSTIC in ('Y','N'))

## REPORT_DESTYPES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains Unique ID for Report Destination Types»; TYPE_DESC VARCHAR2(20)! «This column contains Unique ID for Report Destination Description»; FORMAT_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «Reference key to DEFINITIONS.REPORT_DESFORMAT»; OUTPUT_AT VARCHAR2(10)! «This column contains information of output source e.g. (CACHE, SCREEN, PRINTER, FILE, MAIL etc)»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(2)! «Order by column to manage display order of des types»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contain flag information for Active of inactive state (Y=Active, N=Inactive)»; DEFAULT_TYPE CHAR(1)! «This column contain flag information for destination type marked as defualt (Y=Yes, N=No)»
fk: (FORMAT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.REPORT_DESFORMAT(FORMAT_ID) [disabled]
check: (OUTPUT_AT in ('SCREEN','PRINTER','FILE','CACHE'))
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (DEFAULT_TYPE in ('Y','N'))

## RPC_CPT [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains info about Cpt setup Room Payment Cat wise
cols: RPC_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column contains info about Room Payment Cat Id»; RPCC_SER NUMBER(5)* «This column contains serial no»; RESTRICTION_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(10)! «This column contains info about Restriction Type Id»; ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)! «This col contains info about Organization Id»; VISIT_TYPE CHAR(1)! «This column contains info about Visit Type (F For First, O for Followup)»; DUTY_TIME CHAR(1)! «This column contains info about Duty Time (1 for ON , 0 for Off)»; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)! «This column contains info about CPT»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contains info about Active or In Active (Y for Active, N for In Active)»; ORDER_BY NUMBER(2)! «This column contains info about record priority»

check: (DUTY_TIME in ('0','1'))
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

check: (VISIT_TYPE in ('F','O'))
check: (DUTY_TIME in ('0','1'))
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))

## SCREEN_DISPLAY_MASTER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DISPLAY_ID NUMBER*; DISPLAY_DESC VARCHAR2(200)!; STATUS CHAR(1); DISPLAY_ORDER NUMBER; DISPLAY_TYPE_ID NUMBER; DISPLAY_TIME NUMBER; URL VARCHAR2(500)

fk: (DISPLAY_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SCREEN_DISPLAY_MASTER(DISPLAY_ID) [disabled]

## SEQUENCE_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]

fk: (RECEPTION_ID, LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ORDER_LOCATION_RECEPTION(RECEPTION_ID, LOCATION_ID, ORDER_LOCATION_ID) [disabled]

## SEQ_TOKEN_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SEQ_ID NUMBER(10)*; TOKEN_TYPE_ID NUMBER(16)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1); LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ORDER_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); RECEPTION_ID NUMBER(10)*; ORDERED_BY NUMBER(2)

## SERVER_WISE_APPLICATION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERVER_ID NUMBER(3)*; APPLICATION_ID NUMBER(3)*; START_DATE DATE; END_DATE DATE; ACTIVE CHAR(1)
fk: (SERVER_ID) → DEFINITIONS.APPLICATION_SERVERS(SERVER_ID)
fk: (APPLICATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.APPLICATION_SETUP(APPLICATION_ID) [disabled]

## SERVICE_STATUS [+audit] [+ml] — This table contains definitions of Army Service Status (System Constant Level table)
cols: STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(6)* «Unique Primary Key Status ID»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255)! «This column contains Name/Description of servcies status»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «This column contains flag of Active, Inactive state (Y=Active, N=Inactive)»; RETIRED CHAR(1)! «This column contains flag of Retired status (Y=Retired, N=Non Retired)»; SHORT_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(20)! «This column contains Short description of service status»; CAPTION_IN_NAME VARCHAR2(10) «This column contains caption to be displayed in Full Name of person»; ORDER_NO NUMBER(4)
uk: (DESCRIPTION)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))
check: (RETIRED IN ('N','Y'))

## SERVICE_CATEGORY_RELATION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: CATEGORY_CODE VARCHAR2(20)*; SERVICE_STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(6)!; PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000)
fk: (SERVICE_STATUS_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_STATUS(STATUS_ID) [disabled]
fk: (PATIENT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE(PATIENT_TYPE_ID)
uk: (PATIENT_TYPE_ID, SERVICE_STATUS_ID)

## SERVICE_STATUS_PAT_TYPE
cols: PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; STATUS_ID CHAR(1)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
fk: (PATIENT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE(PATIENT_TYPE_ID)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('N','Y'))

## SERVICE_SUB_GROUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column will represents the Service Type»; THERAPEUTIC_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column will represents the Therapeutic Group»; SUB_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column will represents the Therapeutic Sub Group»; ALL_SERVICE CHAR(1)! «Values of this column must be Y or N, Y means all the Item Group and Items are allowed no need to check from detail level, If N then check from detail tables individually»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
fk: (THERAPEUTIC_GROUP_ID, SUB_GROUP_ID) → PHARMACY.TG_SUBGROUP(T_GROUP_ID, T_SUBGROUP_ID) [disabled]

## SERVICE_THERAPEUTIC_GROUP [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to link the Therapeutic Group with the Service Type
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column will represents the Service Type»; THERAPEUTIC_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «This column will represents the Therapeutic Group»; ALL_SERVICE CHAR(1)! «Values of this column must be Y or N, Y means all the Therapeutic Sub Group, Item Group and Items are allowed no need to check from detail level, If N then check from detail tables individually»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
fk: (THERAPEUTIC_GROUP_ID) → PHARMACY.THERAPEUTIC_GROUP(T_GROUP_ID) [disabled]

## SERVICE_TYPE_CPT [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Service Type wise CPTs
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Service Type Ref»; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «CPT Code»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## SERVICE_TYPE_DEPARTMENT_NATURE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to link the Service Type with the Department Nature
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DEPARTMENT_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ALL_SERVICES CHAR(1)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
fk: (DEPARTMENT_NATURE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT_NATURE(DEPARTMENT_NATURE_ID) [disabled]
check: (ALL_SERVICES IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## SERVICE_TYPE_ITEM [+audit] [+ml] — This Table will be used to define the Service Type wise Item / Brand
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Service Type Ref»; ITEM_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(25)* «Item Group / Generic Ref»; ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(25)* «Item / Brand»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
fk: (ITEM_GROUP_ID) → ITEM.ITEM_GROUP(ITEM_GROUP_ID) [disabled]
fk: (ITEM_ID) → ITEM.ITEM(ITEM_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## SERVICE_TYPE_ITEM_GROUP [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Service Type wise Item Group
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Service Type Ref»; ITEM_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(25)* «Item Group / Generic Ref»; ALL_SERVICES CHAR(1)! «Values must be Y or N, Y means All Item/Brand of this Item Group are included»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
fk: (ITEM_GROUP_ID) → ITEM.ITEM_GROUP(ITEM_GROUP_ID) [disabled]

## SERVICE_TYPE_SECTION_NATURE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Section Nature wise Service Type
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Service Type Ref»; DEPARTMENT_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Department Nature Ref»; SECTION_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Section Nature»; ALL_SERVICES CHAR(1)! «Values must be Y or N, Y means all CPT of this Section Nature are included»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
check: (ALL_SERVICES IN ('Y','N'))
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## SERVICE_TYPE_STORE [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Service Type Stores
cols: SERVICE_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Service Type Ref»; STORE_ID VARCHAR2(25)*; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (SERVICE_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_TYPE(SERVICE_TYPE_ID)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## SESSION_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SESSION_TYPE NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(50); ACTIVE CHAR(1); MARGIN_TIME NUMBER(3)

## SESSIONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SESSION_TYPE NUMBER(3)*; SESSION_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(15); ACTIVE CHAR(1); SESSION_TIME_FROM CHAR(5); SESSION_TIME_TO CHAR(5); SHIFT_TYPE VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (SESSION_TYPE) → DEFINITIONS.SESSION_TYPE(SESSION_TYPE)

## SETUP_DETAIL_ADMIN [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SETUP_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; SETUP_DETAIL_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DEPT_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)!; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ADMIN_BY VARCHAR2(14); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

uk: (DESCRIPTION)

## SETUP_NATURE_ADMIN [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SETUP_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DEPT_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; ADMIN_BY VARCHAR2(14); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

cols: SETUP_DETAIL_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; SETUP_NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); OBJECT_CODE VARCHAR2(11); MODULE_ID VARCHAR2(3); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## SEX [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SEX_ID NUMBER(1)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## SEX_MARITAL_STATUS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SEX_ID NUMBER(1)*; MARITAL_STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(6)*

## SIDE_EFFECT [+audit] [+ml]
cols: EFFECT_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; EFFECT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3); EFFECT_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(2000); REMARKS VARCHAR2(1000); SEQ_NO NUMBER(5); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)
fk: (EFFECT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.EFFECT_TYPE(EFFECT_TYPE_ID) [disabled]

## SINGLE_COLUMN_KEY_TABLES
cols: OWNER VARCHAR2(128)!; CONSTRAINT_NAME VARCHAR2(128)!; TABLE_NAME VARCHAR2(128)!; COLUMN_NAME VARCHAR2(4000); POSITION NUMBER; DATA_TYPE VARCHAR2(60)

## SKILLS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SKILL_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1)

## SNOMED_CODES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DISEASE VARCHAR2(100) «This column contains information of disease»; CODES VARCHAR2(20)* «This column contains Snomed Code (T-Code, M Code) (PK)»; DETAIL1 VARCHAR2(1000) «This column contains other details of snomed code in details1»; DETAIL2 VARCHAR2(100) «This column contains other details of snomed code in details2»; DETAIL3 VARCHAR2(1000) «This column contains other details of snomed code in details2»; COMMENTS VARCHAR2(200) «This column contains other comments field»; PUNJAB_CANCER_REGISTRY CHAR(1) «This column contains flag information of Punjab Cancer Registey»; SER NUMBER(3)* «This column contains subsidoery serial # codes (PK)»; CANCER CHAR(1) «This column contains flag information Cancer Code ('Y' = 'Yes','N' = 'No')»
check: (Punjab_cancer_registry IN ('Y','N'))
check: (Cancer IN ('Y','N'))

## SOFTWARE_CATEGORY [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Category of Software --- It is constant table, reason of this table is that we have many software of same category such as browsers, browser will be defined as category in this table and firefox, IE etc will be defined the child table
cols: SOFTWARE_CATEGORY_ID NUMBER(3)*; CATEGORY_DESC VARCHAR2(50)! «Name of the Software Category such as browsers»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!

## SOFTWARE_VERSION_MASTER [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SOFWARE_ID VARCHAR2(5)* «This column contains info about software ID»; SOFTWARE_NAME VARCHAR2(100) «This column cantains info about software name»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(500) «This column contains info about remarks»; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «This column conatins info about active or in-active»; PATHOLOGY_FLAG CHAR(1) «This column contains info about pathology Flag for bar tender»; SOFTWARE_CATEGORY_ID NUMBER(3)!

## SOFTWARE_VERSION_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SOFWARE_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; SERIAL_NO NUMBER(5)*; INSTALLATION_PATH VARCHAR2(500)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; SOFTWARE_NAME VARCHAR2(100) «This column will be used to define the name of the software»; EXE_NAME VARCHAR2(250) «Name of the executable file»; VESION_NO VARCHAR2(30) «As per Version rule, it can be version name or version number»; SPACE_REQ_AFTER_EXE_NAME CHAR(1)! «When we call servers some browsers needs symbol and some needs space, will handle with the help of this column value»; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3) «This column will contain Multilocation location values for Installation Path.»; SHORT_ORG VARCHAR2(20) «Thio column is used to contains information of short orgnization description»
fk: (SOFWARE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SOFTWARE_VERSION_MASTER(SOFWARE_ID)

cols: SOURCE_CODE VARCHAR2(50)*; SOURCE_CONCEPT_ID NUMBER(10); SOURCE_VOCABULARY_ID VARCHAR2(20)*; SOURCE_CODE_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(4000); TARGET_CONCEPT_ID NUMBER(10)*; TARGET_VOCABULARY_ID VARCHAR2(20)!; VALID_START_DATE DATE!; VALID_END_DATE DATE!; INVALID_REASON VARCHAR2(1); MAPPED_BY VARCHAR2(14); MAPPED_DATE TIMESTAMP(6); APPROVED_BY VARCHAR2(14); APPROVED_DATE TIMESTAMP(6); STATUS VARCHAR2(1); TARGET_CONCEPT_DESC VARCHAR2(500)

cols: SHARE_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Will be autogenerate»; CLINIC_SPECIALTY_ID VARCHAR2(6); ORDERING_SHARE NUMBER; SHARE_TYPE CHAR(1) «F for fixed and V for variable»; PERFORMER_SHARE NUMBER; ACTIVE CHAR(1) «Y mean active N mean not active»; NATURE_ID VARCHAR2(3); NATURE_DETAIL_ID VARCHAR2(3)
uk: (CLINIC_SPECIALTY_ID)

## SPECIALTY_SHARE_DETAIL [+audit] [+ml]

fk: (SHARE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SPECIALTY_SHARE(SHARE_ID) [disabled]

uk: (INSTRUCTION_DESC)

fk: (SPECIMEN_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SPECIMEN(SPECIMEN_ID)
fk: (APPEARANCE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.APPEARANCE(APPEARANCE_ID)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N') )

## SPECIMEN_COLOURS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SPECIMEN_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; COLOUR_ID VARCHAR2(5)*; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)! «Active status Y=Yes,N=No»; DEFAULTS VARCHAR2(1)! «Default selection status Y=Yes,N=No»
fk: (SPECIMEN_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SPECIMEN(SPECIMEN_ID)
fk: (COLOUR_ID) → DEFINITIONS.COLOURS(COLOUR_ID)
check: (ACTIVE in ('Y','N'))
check: (DEFAULTS in ('Y','N'))

cols: COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)*; STATE_ID NUMBER(4)*; NAME VARCHAR2(60); ZONE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; NEW_COUNTRY_ID NUMBER(4)

## STATUSWISE_PATIENT_TYPE [+audit] [+ml]
cols: REC_ID NUMBER*; SERVICE_STATUS_ID VARCHAR2(6); RELATION_ID VARCHAR2(6); OLD_PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6); NEW_PATIENT_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(6)
fk: (NEW_PATIENT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE(PATIENT_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (OLD_PATIENT_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.PATIENT_TYPE(PATIENT_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (RELATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.RELATION(RELATION_ID) [disabled]
fk: (SERVICE_STATUS_ID) → DEFINITIONS.SERVICE_STATUS(STATUS_ID)
uk: (SERVICE_STATUS_ID, RELATION_ID, OLD_PATIENT_TYPE_ID, NEW_PATIENT_TYPE_ID)

## SUB_LOCATIONS [+audit] [+ml] — Any user working in a sub location will have all powers as of its PROJECT/MAIN LOCATION/HEAD OFFICE users. In other words, staff working in a sub location will be considered employee of the same PROJECT/LOCATION/HEAD OFFICE etc.
cols: LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Project or Head Office -- e.g. for SKM 001 will be populated in this field -- This project will be child of some Organization -- SKMT, structure will be defined soon. This value will be assigned to GLOBAL.LOCATION_ID variable on Forms/Backend Session wise Global variables Table at the time of login.»; SUB_LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)* «Offices working under a Project or Main/Head Office -- e.g. C01 JRDC, 007 KDC, self owned CCs etc (But not Franchised CCs) This value will be assigned to GLOBAL.PHYSICAL_LOCATION_ID variable on Forms/Backend Session wise Global variables Table at the time of login.»
fk: (LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)
fk: (SUB_LOCATION_ID) → DEFINITIONS.LOCATION(LOCATION_ID)
uk: (SUB_LOCATION_ID)

## SUB_SERVICES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SUB_SERVICE_ID NUMBER(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(255); SERVICE_CODE CHAR(3)*; INPATIENT CHAR(1); BUSINESS_SERVICES CHAR(1); DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7); TRANS_TYPE CHAR(1); CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18); CASH CHAR(1); NARRATION VARCHAR2(1000); COA_CODE VARCHAR2(100); LEDGER_TYPE_CODE NUMBER(4); SUB_LDGR_ITEM_CODE VARCHAR2(18); CC CHAR(1)! «This Column is added to distinguish Collection Centre Services, If Value is Y then This Service is available for Collection Centre, if N then not Available»
fk: (SERVICE_CODE) → DEFINITIONS.GL_DEPT_SERVICES(SERVICE_CODE)
fk: (DEPARTMENT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DEPARTMENT(DEPARTMENT_ID) [disabled]
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID) [disabled]

## TABLES [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; TABLE_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; NAME VARCHAR2(60)!; TABLE_TYPE_FLAG CHAR(1)!; USELESS_FLAG CHAR(1)!; AUDITING_CHECKED CHAR(1); WHERE_CLAUSE VARCHAR2(4000); WHERE_CLAUSE_2 VARCHAR2(4000); REQUIRED_ON_NEW_LOCATION CHAR(1); PROCEDURE_NAME VARCHAR2(4000); ORDER_BY NUMBER(4); TABLE_LEVEL NUMBER(4); MODEL_TYPE_ID NUMBER(2); OWNER VARCHAR2(60)!; IS_NOT_A_COLLECTION_CENTRE CHAR(1) «CC TYPE»; IS_FRANCHISED_COLLECTION_CNTR CHAR(1) «CC TYPE»; IS_SKM_COLLECTION_CNTR CHAR(1) «CC TYPE»; IS_KIOSKS_CENTRE CHAR(1) «CC TYPE»; WT_TRIGGER_REQUIRED CHAR(1); OLD_TABLE_ID VARCHAR2(4)
fk: (OWNER) → DEFINITIONS.SCHEMAS(NAME)
fk: (MODEL_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.DATA_SYNC_MODEL_TYPES(MODEL_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (TABLE_TYPE_FLAG) → DEFINITIONS.TABLE_TYPES(TABLE_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
fk: (TABLE_TYPE_FLAG, MODEL_TYPE_ID) → DEFINITIONS.TTMT_MATRIX(TABLE_TYPE_ID, MODEL_TYPE_ID) [disabled]
uk: (OWNER, NAME)
check: (NAME = UPPER(NAME))

## TABLES_FMH [+audit] [+ml]
cols: SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3); TABLE_ID VARCHAR2(4); NAME VARCHAR2(30)!; TABLE_TYPE_FLAG CHAR(1); USELESS_FLAG CHAR(1); AUDITING_CHECKED CHAR(1)!; WHERE_CLAUSE VARCHAR2(4000); WHERE_CLAUSE_2 VARCHAR2(4000); REQUIRED_ON_NEW_LOCATION CHAR(1); PROCEDURE_NAME VARCHAR2(4000); ORDER_BY NUMBER(4); TABLE_LEVEL NUMBER(4)

cols: VACCINE_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); DURATION NUMBER; UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(5); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VACCINATION_DEPARTMENT_SECTION
cols: VACCINE_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; SECTION_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); DURATION NUMBER; UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(5); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VACCINATION_DESIGNATION
cols: VACCINE_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(4); DESIGNATION_ID VARCHAR2(6); DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7); DURATION NUMBER; UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(5); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VACCINATION_DOSE_SCHEDULES [+audit] — This table is used to store the information of vaccine schedules.
cols: VACCINE_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; SCHEDULE_NO NUMBER* «This column is used to store the Dose# of vaccine.»; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(100); DOSE_GAP NUMBER «This column is used to store the dose gap for the next dose to be administer.»; PATIENT_TRANSFER_QUEST VARCHAR2(4000) «This column is used to store the patient transfer question description.»; PATIENT_DISCHAGED_QUEST_1 VARCHAR2(4000) «This column is used to store the discharge question no. 1 description.»; PATIENT_DISCHAGED_QUEST_2 VARCHAR2(4000) «This column is used to store the discharge question no. 2 description.»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1) «Y - Yes, N - No.»; WS_SYNC_DATE DATE; VALIDITY NUMBER

## VACCINATION_EMPLOYEES
cols: VACCINE_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; MRNO VARCHAR2(14)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3); DURATION NUMBER; UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(5); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VACCINATION_GROUP
cols: VACCINE_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(150); VACCINE_DOSE_DAY NUMBER(6); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VACCINATION_INSTRUCTIONS [+audit] — This table is used to store the information of vaccine instructions.
cols: VACCINE_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; INSTRUCTION_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)*; SR_NO NUMBER*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(100); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1) «Y - Yes, N - No.»; WS_SYNC_DATE DATE

## VACCINATION_LOCATIONS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: VACCINE_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; ORGANIZATION_ID VARCHAR2(7)*; LOCATION_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; VACCINE_RESPONSIBILITY VARCHAR2(1); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1); PENDING_DURATION NUMBER «Needle Stick & Vaccination Pending List wil display befor Nth no of days. (Needle Stick & Vaccination Pending Duration in Days. System Constant - 316)»; FOLLOWUP_DURATION NUMBER «Needle Stick & Vaccination followup email will be send before given N days. (Needle Stick & Vaccination Followup Email Sending Duration. System Constant 317)»; EXTENSION_NO VARCHAR2(100) «Extension No, In case of any query regarding Needle Stick/Vaccination. (Extension No, In case of any query regarding Needle Stick/Vaccination. System Constant - 524)»; VACCINATION_TIME VARCHAR2(200) «Vaccination time (Vaccination time will be mentioned in system constant value. System Constant - 1025)»; VACCINATION_ROOM VARCHAR2(200) «Vaccination room (Vaccination room will be mentioned in system constant value. System Constant - 1026)»

## VACCINATION_QUESTIONS [+audit] — This table is used to store the vaccine questions for patient evaluation.
cols: VACCINE_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; QUESTION_TYPE VARCHAR2(3)* «This column is used to store type of question. 001 - Evaluation 002- checklist»; QUESTION_NO NUMBER* «This column is used to store the serial number of question»; PARENT_QUESTION_NO NUMBER; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(100); AUTO_FILL VARCHAR2(1); REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1) «Y - Yes, N - No.»; WS_SYNC_DATE DATE

## VACCINATION_ROLE
cols: VACCINE_GROUP_ID VARCHAR2(4)*; ROLE_ID NUMBER(10)*; DURATION NUMBER; UNIT_ID VARCHAR2(5); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VISIT_LOCATION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: VISIT_LOC_ID NUMBER*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(100); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VISIT_LOC_LANGUAGE_SETUP [+audit] [+ml]
cols: LANGUGAE_ID VARCHAR2(6)*; VISIT_LOC_ID NUMBER*; LABEL_DESC NVARCHAR2(500) «Label Desc field will be used to show langugae wise text on Discharge Instruction report on discharge card»; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## VITAL_SIGNS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: VS_ID VARCHAR2(30)*; VS_DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(200)!; ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; VS_SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(200)!
uk: (VS_DESCRIPTION)

## VRIII_PROTOCOL_SETUP [+audit]
cols: SR_NO NUMBER* «This column contains Unique indetifier»; BSL_MIN_RANGE NUMBER «This column contains value for minimum range»; BSL_MAX_RANGE NUMBER «This column contains value for maximum range»; BSL_RANGE_DESC VARCHAR2(15) «This column contains BSL range value»; INSULIN_VALUE NUMBER(5,2) «This column contains BSL value for specific range»; REMARKS VARCHAR2(4000)

## VRIII_SUBSTRATE_SETS
cols: SUBSTRATE_SET_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); ORDER_NO NUMBER; ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## WALKING_AID [+audit] [+ml]
cols: AID_ID VARCHAR2(8)*; DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500); ACTIVE VARCHAR2(1)

## WARD_WISE_CPT [+audit] [+ml] — This table will be used to define the Ward wise CPTs
cols: ROOM_ID VARCHAR2(7)* «Ward Id for which below CPT(s) will be charged»; CPT_ID VARCHAR2(18)* «CPT Code which will be charged against Ward»; TRANS_DATE DATE! «Date when transaction was made»; ACTIVE CHAR(1)! «Status of the Transaction»
fk: (ROOM_ID) → DEFINITIONS.ROOMS(ROOM_ID)
fk: (CPT_ID) → DEFINITIONS.CPT(CPT_ID) [disabled]
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))

## WEB_REPORT_SERVER_R [+audit] [+ml]
cols: REPORT_SERVER_NAME VARCHAR2(30); REPORT_SERVER_DOMAIN VARCHAR2(30); ACTIVE CHAR(1)

## WHISPER_DATASET
cols: ID NUMBER; AUDIO_BLOB BLOB; TEXT CLOB!; STATUS VARCHAR2(50); LOCKED_BY VARCHAR2(100); LOCK_TIMESTAMP TIMESTAMP(6); UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP(6)

## WHISPER_DATASET_SYNTH
cols: ID NUMBER*; AUDIO_BLOB BLOB; TEXT CLOB!; STATUS VARCHAR2(50); LOCKED_BY VARCHAR2(100); LOCK_TIMESTAMP TIMESTAMP(6); UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP(6)

## WHISPER_DATASET_SYNTH_CLEANED
cols: ID NUMBER!; TEXT CLOB!

## WHISPER_DATASET_SYNTH_COMPLETED
cols: ID NUMBER!; AUDIO_BLOB BLOB; TEXT CLOB!

## WORKFLOW_NOTE_EXEMPTION [+audit] [+ml]
cols: PURCHASE_TYPE_ID NUMBER(3)*; SCHEMA_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; WORK_FLOW_ID NUMBER(4)*; EVENT_ID NUMBER(3)*

## WORKING_AREA [+audit] [+ml]
cols: DEPARTMENT_ID VARCHAR2(7); SECTION_ID VARCHAR2(7); WORKING_AREA_ID VARCHAR2(5); DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(60); ACTIVE CHAR(1)!; ORDER_BY NUMBER(3)
check: (ACTIVE IN ('Y','N'))
check: (DESCRIPTION IS NOT NULL)

## WORK_FLOW_EVENTS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: WORK_FLOW_ID NUMBER(4)*; TRANSACTION_TYPE_ID VARCHAR2(3)*; EVENT_ID VARCHAR2(4)*
fk: (WORK_FLOW_ID) → DEFINITIONS.WORK_FLOW(WORK_FLOW_ID)

## YEARS [+audit] [+ml]
cols: START_DATE DATE!; END_DATE DATE!; FLAG VARCHAR2(1)
